FILE: Pocket Ledger/Views/Analysis/ReconcileView.swift
==================================================

import SwiftUI

struct ReconcileInputView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @Environment(\.dismiss) var dismiss
    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    
    let selectedIDs: Set<UUID>
    
    // User Input: Maps Target ID (Account or Group) -> Input String
    @State private var balanceInputs: [UUID: String] = [:]
    
    // Logic: Which target is currently being fixed?
    @State private var targetToAdjust: ReconcileTarget?
    @State private var differenceToAdjust: Double = 0
    
    // Sheet States
    @State private var showingAdjustmentSheet = false
    
    // Model B Logic: If adjusting a group, we need to pick an account
    @State private var showingGroupTargetPicker = false
    @State private var selectedAccountForGroupAdjustment: Account?
    
    // MARK: - Unified Target Model
    enum ReconcileTarget: Identifiable, Hashable {
        case account(Account)
        case group(AccountGroup)
        
        var id: UUID {
            switch self {
            case .account(let a): return a.id
            case .group(let g): return g.id
            }
        }
        
        var name: String {
            switch self {
            case .account(let a): return a.name
            case .group(let g): return g.name
            }
        }
        
        // Helper to get system balance
        func systemBalance(in data: AppData) -> Double {
            switch self {
            case .account(let a): return data.balance(for: a)
            case .group(let g): return data.groupBalance(g)
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Computed list of targets based on selection
    private var targets: [ReconcileTarget] {
        var result: [ReconcileTarget] = []
        
        for id in selectedIDs {
            if let group = data.accountGroups.first(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                result.append(.group(group))
            } else if let account = data.accounts.first(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                result.append(.account(account))
            }
        }
        // Sort by name
        return result.sorted { $0.name < $1.name }
    }

    var body: some View {
        List {
            // Instructions
            Section {
                Text("Enter the actual total balance for each selected item.")
                    .font(.caption)
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
            }
            
            // The Input List
            ForEach(targets) { target in
                targetRow(target)
            }
        }
        .navigationTitle("Reconcile")
        .toolbar {
            ToolbarItem(placement: .primaryAction) {
                Button("Done") {
                    dismiss()
                }
            }
        }
        // SHEET 1: Adjustment Details (Category, Notes)
        .sheet(isPresented: $showingAdjustmentSheet) {
            if let target = targetToAdjust {
                AdjustmentDetailSheet(
                    targetName: target.name,
                    difference: differenceToAdjust,
                    currencySymbol: currencySymbol,
                    onConfirm: { categoryID in
                        finalizeAdjustment(categoryID: categoryID)
                    }
                )
            }
        }
        // SHEET 2: Model B Specific - Pick Account for Group Adjustment
        .confirmationDialog("Apply Adjustment To...", isPresented: $showingGroupTargetPicker, titleVisibility: .visible) {
            if case .group(let group) = targetToAdjust {
                let eligibleAccounts = getAccountsRecursive(for: group)
                
                ForEach(eligibleAccounts) { account in
                    Button(account.name) {
                        selectedAccountForGroupAdjustment = account
                        // Once account is picked, proceed to standard adjustment sheet
                        showingAdjustmentSheet = true
                    }
                }
                
                Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) {
                    targetToAdjust = nil
                }
            }
        } message: {
            Text("Since you are reconciling a group, please select which account contains the discrepancy.")
        }
        .decimalPadToolbar()
        .onAppear {
            initializeInputs()
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Row Component
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private func targetRow(_ target: ReconcileTarget) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            // Header
            HStack {
                if case .group = target {
                    Image(systemName: "folder.fill").foregroundColor(.purple)
                } else {
                    Image(systemName: "building.columns").foregroundColor(.blue)
                }
                Text(target.name).font(.headline)
            }
            
            // Input Area
            HStack(spacing: 16) {
                VStack(alignment: .leading) {
                    Text("System").font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    Text(target.systemBalance(in: data).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                        .font(.subheadline)
                        .monospacedDigit()
                }
                
                Image(systemName: "arrow.right").font(.caption).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                
                VStack(alignment: .leading) {
                    Text("Actual").font(.caption2).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    TextField("0.00", text: Binding(
                        get: { balanceInputs[target.id] ?? "" },
                        set: { balanceInputs[target.id] = $0 }
                    ))
                    .keyboardType(.decimalPad)
                    .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)
                    .frame(width: 100)
                }
                
                Spacer()
            }
            
            // Difference & Action
            if let diff = calculateDifference(for: target), abs(diff) > 0.01 {
                HStack {
                    Text("Diff: \(diff > 0 ? "+" : "")\(diff.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))")
                        .font(.subheadline)
                        .foregroundColor(diff > 0 ? .green : .red)
                    
                    Spacer()
                    
                    Button("Fix") {
                        startFixProcess(for: target, difference: diff)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.borderedProminent)
                    .font(.caption)
                }
                .padding(8)
                .background(Color.secondary.opacity(0.1))
                .cornerRadius(8)
            } else if let input = balanceInputs[target.id], !input.isEmpty {
                 HStack {
                    Image(systemName: "checkmark.circle.fill").foregroundColor(.green)
                    Text("Balanced").font(.caption).foregroundColor(.green)
                }
            }
        }
        .padding(.vertical, 4)
    }
    
    // MARK: - Logic
    
    private func initializeInputs() {
        for target in targets {
            let balance = target.systemBalance(in: data)
            balanceInputs[target.id] = String(format: "%.2f", balance)
        }
    }
    
    private func calculateDifference(for target: ReconcileTarget) -> Double? {
        guard let text = balanceInputs[target.id], let actual = Double(text) else { return nil }
        return actual - target.systemBalance(in: data)
    }
    
    private func startFixProcess(for target: ReconcileTarget, difference: Double) {
        targetToAdjust = target
        differenceToAdjust = difference
        
        switch target {
        case .account(let account):
            // Simple case: We know the account
            selectedAccountForGroupAdjustment = account
            showingAdjustmentSheet = true
            
        case .group:
            // Model B case: We must ask user for the account
            showingGroupTargetPicker = true
        }
    }
    
    private func finalizeAdjustment(categoryID: UUID?) {
        guard let account = selectedAccountForGroupAdjustment else { return }
        
        // Create Transaction
        let type: TransactionType = differenceToAdjust > 0 ? .inflow : .outflow
        
        // Auto-category fallback
        var finalCatID = categoryID
        if finalCatID == nil {
            finalCatID = data.categories.first(where: { $0.name == "Adjustment" || $0.name == "Other" })?.id
            if finalCatID == nil { finalCatID = data.categories.first?.id }
        }
        
        if let catID = finalCatID {
            data.addTransaction(
                type: type,
                accountID: account.id,
                categoryID: catID,
                tagIDs: [],
                amount: abs(differenceToAdjust),
                date: Date(),
                isOutlier: true
            )
        }
        
        // Reset Input for this target to match new reality
        if let target = targetToAdjust {
            // We update the input to match the (now corrected) system balance
            // effectively "clearing" the error visually
            balanceInputs[target.id] = nil
        }
        
        // Cleanup
        targetToAdjust = nil
        selectedAccountForGroupAdjustment = nil
    }
    
    // Recursive helper to find all concrete accounts inside a group
    private func getAccountsRecursive(for group: AccountGroup) -> [Account] {
        var results: [Account] = []
        let allIDs = group.allAccountIDs(in: data.accountGroups, accounts: data.accounts)
        for id in allIDs {
            if let acc = data.accounts.first(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                results.append(acc)
            }
        }
        return results
    }
}

// Reuse the existing AdjustmentDetailSheet (it is generic enough)
// Minor tweak: pass `targetName` string instead of `Account` object to make it compatible
struct AdjustmentDetailSheet: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @Environment(\.dismiss) var dismiss
    
    let targetName: String
    let difference: Double
    let currencySymbol: String
    let onConfirm: (UUID?) -> Void
    
    @State private var selectedCategoryID: UUID?
    @State private var markAsOutlier: Bool = true
    
    private var transactionType: TransactionType {
        difference > 0 ? .inflow : .outflow
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                Section(header: Text("Adjustment Summary")) {
                    HStack {
                        Text("Target")
                        Spacer()
                        Text(targetName).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                    HStack {
                        Text("Amount")
                        Spacer()
                        Text(abs(difference).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                            .fontWeight(.bold)
                    }
                }
                
                Section(header: Text("Category")) {
                    Picker("Category", selection: $selectedCategoryID) {
                        Text("Auto-select").tag(nil as UUID?)
                        ForEach(data.categories.filter { $0.type.rawValue == transactionType.rawValue }) { cat in
                            Text(cat.emoji + " " + cat.name).tag(cat.id as UUID?)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle("Confirm Adjustment")
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .confirmationAction) {
                    Button("Create") {
                        onConfirm(selectedCategoryID)
                        dismiss()
                    }
                }
                ToolbarItem(placement: .cancellationAction) {
                    Button("Cancel") { dismiss() }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: Pocket Ledger/Views/Analysis/FilterSheet.swift
==================================================

import SwiftUI

struct FilterSheet: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @Environment(\.dismiss) var dismiss

    @Binding var excludedAccountIDs: Set<UUID>
    @Binding var excludedCategoryIDs: Set<UUID>
    @Binding var excludedTagIDs: Set<UUID>
    @Binding var excludeOutliers: Bool
    
    // NEW: Amount filter bindings
    @Binding var minimumAmount: Double?
    @Binding var maximumAmount: Double?
    
    // State for saving preset
    @State private var showingSaveSheet = false
    @State private var newPresetName = ""
    
    // Amount filter UI state
    @State private var minAmountString = ""
    @State private var maxAmountString = ""
    
    // Filter mode
    enum FilterMode {
        case simple
        case advanced
    }
    @State private var filterMode: FilterMode = .simple
    
    // Simple mode selections (inclusion-based)
    @State private var selectedAccountIDs: Set<UUID> = []
    @State private var selectedCategoryIDs: Set<UUID> = []
    @State private var selectedTagIDs: Set<UUID> = []
    
    // Computed properties
    private var hasActiveFilters: Bool {
        !excludedAccountIDs.isEmpty || !excludedCategoryIDs.isEmpty || !excludedTagIDs.isEmpty ||
        excludeOutliers || minimumAmount != nil || maximumAmount != nil
    }
    
    private var filterSummary: String {
        var parts: [String] = []
        
        let includedAccounts = data.accounts.count - excludedAccountIDs.count
        if excludedAccountIDs.count > 0 {
            parts.append("\(includedAccounts) of \(data.accounts.count) accounts")
        }
        
        let includedCategories = data.categories.count - excludedCategoryIDs.count
        if excludedCategoryIDs.count > 0 {
            parts.append("\(includedCategories) of \(data.categories.count) categories")
        }
        
        if !excludedTagIDs.isEmpty {
            parts.append("Excluding \(excludedTagIDs.count) tags")
        }
        
        if excludeOutliers {
            parts.append("Excluding outliers")
        }
        
        if let min = minimumAmount {
            parts.append("Min: \(min.formattedCurrency(symbol: "$"))")
        }
        
        if let max = maximumAmount {
            parts.append("Max: \(max.formattedCurrency(symbol: "$"))")
        }
        
        return parts.isEmpty ? "All data included" : parts.joined(separator: " • ")
    }

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            List {
                // Mode Picker
                Section(footer: Text(filterMode == .simple ?
                         "Choose what to include in your view" :
                         "Fine-tune by excluding specific items")) {
                    Picker("Filter Mode", selection: $filterMode) {
                        Text("Simple").tag(FilterMode.simple)
                        Text("Advanced").tag(FilterMode.advanced)
                    }
                    .pickerStyle(.segmented)
                }
                
                if filterMode == .simple {
                    simpleFilterUI
                } else {
                    advancedFilterUI
                }
                
                // Summary Section
                if hasActiveFilters {
                    Section(header: Text("Active Filters")) {
                        Text(filterSummary)
                            .font(.caption)
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        
                        Button("Clear All Filters") {
                            resetFilters()
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(.red)
                    }
                }
                
                // Spacer for bottom toolbar
                Color.clear.frame(height: 80).listRowBackground(Color.clear)
            }
            .navigationTitle("Filter Data")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .cancellationAction) {
                    Button("Cancel") { dismiss() }
                }
            }
            .safeAreaInset(edge: .bottom) {
                VStack(spacing: 0) {
                    Divider()
                    
                    HStack(spacing: 12) {
                        // Save as View button (secondary)
                        if hasActiveFilters {
                            Button {
                                showingSaveSheet = true
                            } label: {
                                HStack {
                                    Image(systemName: "bookmark")
                                    Text("Save View")
                                }
                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                .padding()
                                .background(Color(.secondarySystemBackground))
                                .foregroundColor(.blue)
                                .cornerRadius(12)
                            }
                        }
                        
                        // Apply button (primary)
                        Button {
                            if filterMode == .simple {
                                convertSimpleToExclusion()
                            }
                            dismiss()
                        } label: {
                            Text("Apply Filters")
                                .fontWeight(.semibold)
                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                .padding()
                                .background(Color.blue)
                                .foregroundColor(.white)
                                .cornerRadius(12)
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                    .padding(.vertical, 12)
                    .background(Color(.systemBackground))
                }
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: $showingSaveSheet) {
                SavePresetSheet(
                    excludedAccountIDs: excludedAccountIDs,
                    excludedCategoryIDs: excludedCategoryIDs,
                    excludedTagIDs: excludedTagIDs,
                    excludeOutliers: excludeOutliers,
                    onSave: { name in
                        let preset = InsightPreset(
                            name: name,
                            excludedAccountIDs: excludedAccountIDs,
                            excludedCategoryIDs: excludedCategoryIDs,
                            excludedTagIDs: excludedTagIDs,
                            excludeOutliers: excludeOutliers
                        )
                        data.savePreset(preset)
                        dismiss()
                    }
                )
            }
            .onAppear {
                initializeSimpleSelections()
                initializeAmountStrings()
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Simple Filter UI
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private var simpleFilterUI: some View {
        // Quick Filters
        Section(header: Text("Quick Filters")) {
            QuickFilterButton(
                icon: "checkmark.circle.fill",
                title: "All Data",
                isActive: !hasActiveFilters
            ) {
                resetFilters()
            }
            
            QuickFilterButton(
                icon: "creditcard",
                title: "Only Debit Accounts",
                isActive: false
            ) {
                applyDebitOnlyFilter()
            }
            
            QuickFilterButton(
                icon: "chart.bar",
                title: "Exclude Outliers",
                isActive: excludeOutliers
            ) {
                excludeOutliers.toggle()
            }
            
            QuickFilterButton(
                icon: "dollarsign.circle",
                title: "Large Expenses Only (>$100)",
                isActive: minimumAmount == 100
            ) {
                applyLargeExpensesFilter()
            }
        }
        
        // Amount Filters
        Section(header: Text("Amount Range (Optional)"), footer: Text("Filter transactions by amount. Useful for excluding small purchases or focusing on large expenses.")) {
            HStack {
                Text("Minimum")
                Spacer()
                TextField("No limit", text: $minAmountString)
                    .keyboardType(.decimalPad)
                    .multilineTextAlignment(.trailing)
                    .frame(width: 100)
                    .onChange(of: minAmountString) { newValue in
                        minimumAmount = Double(newValue)
                    }
            }
            
            HStack {
                Text("Maximum")
                Spacer()
                TextField("No limit", text: $maxAmountString)
                    .keyboardType(.decimalPad)
                    .multilineTextAlignment(.trailing)
                    .frame(width: 100)
                    .onChange(of: maxAmountString) { newValue in
                        maximumAmount = Double(newValue)
                    }
            }
        }
        
        // Account Selection
        if !data.accounts.isEmpty {
            Section(header: Text("Include Accounts"), footer: Text("Select accounts to include in this view. Unselected accounts will be excluded.")) {
                ForEach(accountsByType, id: \.type) { section in
                    DisclosureGroup(
                        isExpanded: Binding(
                            get: { selectedAccountIDs.contains(where: { id in section.accounts.contains(where: { $0.id == id }) }) },
                            set: { _ in }
                        )
                    ) {
                        ForEach(section.accounts) { account in
                            Button {
                                toggleSimpleSelection(account.id, in: &selectedAccountIDs)
                            } label: {
                                HStack {
                                    Image(systemName: account.accountType.icon)
                                        .foregroundColor(accountTypeColor(account.accountType))
                                    Text(account.name)
                                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                                    Spacer()
                                    if selectedAccountIDs.contains(account.id) {
                                        Image(systemName: "checkmark.circle.fill")
                                            .foregroundColor(.blue)
                                    } else {
                                        Image(systemName: "circle")
                                            .foregroundColor(.secondary.opacity(0.3))
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    } label: {
                        HStack {
                            Text(section.type.rawValue)
                                .fontWeight(.medium)
                            Spacer()
                            let selectedCount = section.accounts.filter { selectedAccountIDs.contains($0.id) }.count
                            Text("\(selectedCount)/\(section.accounts.count)")
                                .font(.caption)
                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        // Category Selection (Collapsed by default, only show if user wants to filter)
        Section(header: Text("Categories (Optional)")) {
            DisclosureGroup("Filter by Categories") {
                Button("Include All Categories") {
                    selectedCategoryIDs = Set(data.categories.map { $0.id })
                }
                .font(.caption)
                
                ForEach(data.categories) { category in
                    Button {
                        toggleSimpleSelection(category.id, in: &selectedCategoryIDs)
                    } label: {
                        HStack {
                            Text(category.emoji)
                            Text(category.name)
                                .foregroundColor(.primary)
                            Spacer()
                            if selectedCategoryIDs.contains(category.id) {
                                Image(systemName: "checkmark.circle.fill")
                                    .foregroundColor(.blue)
                            } else {
                                Image(systemName: "circle")
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary.opacity(0.3))
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Advanced Filter UI
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private var advancedFilterUI: some View {
        Section(header: Text("General")) {
            Toggle("Exclude Outlier Transactions", isOn: $excludeOutliers)
        }
        
        // Amount Range
        Section(header: Text("Amount Range")) {
            HStack {
                Text("Minimum")
                Spacer()
                TextField("No limit", text: $minAmountString)
                    .keyboardType(.decimalPad)
                    .multilineTextAlignment(.trailing)
                    .frame(width: 100)
                    .onChange(of: minAmountString) { newValue in
                        minimumAmount = Double(newValue)
                    }
            }
            
            HStack {
                Text("Maximum")
                Spacer()
                TextField("No limit", text: $maxAmountString)
                    .keyboardType(.decimalPad)
                    .multilineTextAlignment(.trailing)
                    .frame(width: 100)
                    .onChange(of: maxAmountString) { newValue in
                        maximumAmount = Double(newValue)
                    }
            }
        }
        
        Section(header: Text("Exclude Accounts")) {
            if excludedAccountIDs.isEmpty {
                Text("All accounts included")
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    .font(.caption)
            }
            
            ForEach(data.accounts) { account in
                Button {
                    toggleExclusion(account.id, in: &excludedAccountIDs)
                } label: {
                    HStack {
                        Image(systemName: account.accountType.icon)
                            .foregroundColor(accountTypeColor(account.accountType))
                        Text(account.name)
                            .foregroundColor(.primary)
                        Spacer()
                        if excludedAccountIDs.contains(account.id) {
                            Image(systemName: "xmark.circle.fill")
                                .foregroundColor(.red)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        Section(header: Text("Exclude Categories")) {
            if excludedCategoryIDs.isEmpty {
                Text("All categories included")
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    .font(.caption)
            }
            
            ForEach(data.categories) { category in
                Button {
                    toggleExclusion(category.id, in: &excludedCategoryIDs)
                } label: {
                    HStack {
                        Text(category.emoji)
                        Text(category.name)
                            .foregroundColor(.primary)
                        Spacer()
                        if excludedCategoryIDs.contains(category.id) {
                            Image(systemName: "xmark.circle.fill")
                                .foregroundColor(.red)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        if !data.tags.isEmpty {
            Section(header: Text("Exclude Tags")) {
                if excludedTagIDs.isEmpty {
                    Text("All tags included")
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        .font(.caption)
                }
                
                ForEach(data.tags) { tag in
                    Button {
                        toggleExclusion(tag.id, in: &excludedTagIDs)
                    } label: {
                        HStack {
                            Text(tag.name)
                                .foregroundColor(.primary)
                            Spacer()
                            if excludedTagIDs.contains(tag.id) {
                                Image(systemName: "xmark.circle.fill")
                                    .foregroundColor(.red)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Helpers
    
    private var accountsByType: [(type: AccountType, accounts: [Account])] {
        let typeOrder: [AccountType] = [.debit, .goal, .budget, .debt]
        
        return typeOrder.compactMap { type in
            let accountsOfType = data.accounts
                .filter { $0.accountType == type }
                .sorted { $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveCompare($1.name) == .orderedAscending }
            return accountsOfType.isEmpty ? nil : (type, accountsOfType)
        }
    }
    
    private func accountTypeColor(_ type: AccountType) -> Color {
        switch type {
        case .debit:  return .primary
        case .goal:   return .blue
        case .budget: return .purple
        case .debt:   return .orange
        }
    }
    
    private func toggleSimpleSelection(_ id: UUID, in set: inout Set<UUID>) {
        if set.contains(id) {
            set.remove(id)
        } else {
            set.insert(id)
        }
    }
    
    private func toggleExclusion(_ id: UUID, in set: inout Set<UUID>) {
        if set.contains(id) {
            set.remove(id)
        } else {
            set.insert(id)
        }
    }
    
    private func initializeSimpleSelections() {
        // Convert current exclusions to inclusions
        selectedAccountIDs = Set(data.accounts.map { $0.id }).subtracting(excludedAccountIDs)
        selectedCategoryIDs = Set(data.categories.map { $0.id }).subtracting(excludedCategoryIDs)
        selectedTagIDs = Set(data.tags.map { $0.id }).subtracting(excludedTagIDs)
    }
    
    private func convertSimpleToExclusion() {
        // Convert inclusions back to exclusions
        excludedAccountIDs = Set(data.accounts.map { $0.id }).subtracting(selectedAccountIDs)
        excludedCategoryIDs = Set(data.categories.map { $0.id }).subtracting(selectedCategoryIDs)
        excludedTagIDs = Set(data.tags.map { $0.id }).subtracting(selectedTagIDs)
    }
    
    private func resetFilters() {
        excludedAccountIDs.removeAll()
        excludedCategoryIDs.removeAll()
        excludedTagIDs.removeAll()
        excludeOutliers = false
        minimumAmount = nil
        maximumAmount = nil
        minAmountString = ""
        maxAmountString = ""
        initializeSimpleSelections()
    }
    
    private func applyDebitOnlyFilter() {
        let debitAccountIDs = data.accounts.filter { !$0.isCredit }.map { $0.id }
        selectedAccountIDs = Set(debitAccountIDs)
    }
    
    private func applyLargeExpensesFilter() {
        if minimumAmount == 100 {
            // Toggle off
            minimumAmount = nil
            minAmountString = ""
        } else {
            // Toggle on
            minimumAmount = 100
            minAmountString = "100"
        }
    }
    
    private func initializeAmountStrings() {
        if let min = minimumAmount {
            minAmountString = String(format: "%.2f", min)
        }
        if let max = maximumAmount {
            maxAmountString = String(format: "%.2f", max)
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Quick Filter Button

struct QuickFilterButton: View {
    let icon: String
    let title: String
    let isActive: Bool
    let action: () -> Void
    
    var body: some View {
        Button(action: action) {
            HStack {
                Image(systemName: icon)
                    .foregroundColor(isActive ? .blue : .secondary)
                Text(title)
                    .foregroundColor(.primary)
                Spacer()
                if isActive {
                    Image(systemName: "checkmark")
                        .foregroundColor(.blue)
                        .fontWeight(.bold)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Save Preset Sheet

struct SavePresetSheet: View {
    @Environment(\.dismiss) var dismiss
    
    let excludedAccountIDs: Set<UUID>
    let excludedCategoryIDs: Set<UUID>
    let excludedTagIDs: Set<UUID>
    let excludeOutliers: Bool
    let onSave: (String) -> Void
    
    @State private var presetName = ""
    @FocusState private var isNameFieldFocused: Bool
    
    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                Section(header: Text("View Name")) {
                    TextField("E.g., Monthly Spending, Debit Only", text: $presetName)
                        .focused($isNameFieldFocused)
                        .submitLabel(.done)
                        .onSubmit {
                            if canSave {
                                saveAndDismiss()
                            }
                        }
                }
                
                Section(header: Text("This View Includes")) {
                    if excludedAccountIDs.isEmpty && excludedCategoryIDs.isEmpty && excludedTagIDs.isEmpty && !excludeOutliers {
                        Text("All transactions")
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    } else {
                        if !excludedAccountIDs.isEmpty {
                            Text("Excludes \(excludedAccountIDs.count) account(s)")
                                .font(.caption)
                        }
                        if !excludedCategoryIDs.isEmpty {
                            Text("Excludes \(excludedCategoryIDs.count) category(ies)")
                                .font(.caption)
                        }
                        if !excludedTagIDs.isEmpty {
                            Text("Excludes \(excludedTagIDs.count) tag(s)")
                                .font(.caption)
                        }
                        if excludeOutliers {
                            Text("Excludes outlier transactions")
                                .font(.caption)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle("Save View")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .cancellationAction) {
                    Button("Cancel") {
                        dismiss()
                    }
                }
                ToolbarItem(placement: .confirmationAction) {
                    Button("Save") {
                        saveAndDismiss()
                    }
                    .disabled(!canSave)
                    .fontWeight(.semibold)
                }
            }
            .onAppear {
                isNameFieldFocused = true
            }
        }
        .presentationDetents([.medium])
    }
    
    private var canSave: Bool {
        !presetName.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty
    }
    
    private func saveAndDismiss() {
        onSave(presetName)
        dismiss()
    }
}

FILE: Pocket Ledger/Views/Analysis/ExportView.swift

import SwiftUI
import UniformTypeIdentifiers

// MARK: - CSV Document
struct CSVDocument: FileDocument {
    static var readableContentTypes: [UTType] { [.commaSeparatedText] }

    var text: String

    init(text: String) {
        self.text = text
    }

    init(configuration: ReadConfiguration) throws {
        self.text = ""
    }

    func fileWrapper(configuration: WriteConfiguration) throws -> FileWrapper {
        let data = Data(text.utf8)
        return FileWrapper(regularFileWithContents: data)
    }
}

// MARK: - JSON Document
struct JSONBackupDocument: FileDocument {
    static var readableContentTypes: [UTType] { [.json] }
    
    var savedData: SavedData
    
    init(data: SavedData) {
        self.savedData = data
    }
    
    init(configuration: ReadConfiguration) throws {
        let data = configuration.file.regularFileContents ?? Data()
        self.savedData = try JSONDecoder().decode(SavedData.self, from: data)
    }
    
    func fileWrapper(configuration: WriteConfiguration) throws -> FileWrapper {
        let encoder = JSONEncoder()
        encoder.outputFormatting = .prettyPrinted
        let data = try encoder.encode(savedData)
        return FileWrapper(regularFileWithContents: data)
    }
}

// MARK: - Export View
struct ExportView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @Environment(\.dismiss) var dismiss
    
    // UI State
    @State private var exportFormat: ExportFormat = .csv
    
    // CSV State
    @State private var startDate: Date = Calendar.current.date(byAdding: .month, value: -1, to: Date()) ?? Date()
    @State private var endDate: Date = Date()
    
    // Exporter Triggers
    @State private var showCSVExporter = false
    @State private var showJSONExporter = false
    
    // Alert State
    @State private var showErrorAlert = false
    @State private var errorMessage = ""
    
    // Documents
    @State private var csvDocument = CSVDocument(text: "")
    @State private var jsonDocument = JSONBackupDocument(data: SavedData(
        accounts: [],
        accountGroups: [],
        categories: [],
        tags: [],
        transactions: [],
        templates: [],
        reminderTime: nil,
        insightPresets: []
    ))

    enum ExportFormat: String, CaseIterable, Identifiable {
        case csv = "CSV Report"
        case json = "JSON Backup"
        var id: String { self.rawValue }
    }

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                Section {
                    Picker("Format", selection: $exportFormat) {
                        ForEach(ExportFormat.allCases) { format in
                            Text(format.rawValue).tag(format)
                        }
                    }
                    .pickerStyle(SegmentedPickerStyle())
                }
                
                if exportFormat == .csv {
                    Section(header: Text("Date Range"), footer: Text("Exports visible transactions only.")) {
                        DatePicker("Start Date", selection: $startDate, displayedComponents: [.date])
                        DatePicker("End Date", selection: $endDate, displayedComponents: [.date])
                    }
                } else {
                    Section(header: Text("Backup Summary"), footer: Text("Creates a full backup file of all accounts, transactions, templates, and settings.")) {
                        summaryRow(label: "Transactions", value: "\(data.transactions.count)")
                        summaryRow(label: "Accounts", value: "\(data.accounts.count)")
                        summaryRow(label: "Templates", value: "\(data.templates.count)")
                        summaryRow(label: "Categories", value: "\(data.categories.count)")
                        summaryRow(label: "Tags", value: "\(data.tags.count)")
                    }
                }
                
                Section {
                    Button {
                        if exportFormat == .csv {
                            prepareAndExportCSV()
                        } else {
                            prepareAndExportJSON()
                        }
                    } label: {
                        HStack {
                            Spacer()
                            Text(exportFormat == .csv ? "Export CSV" : "Export Backup")
                                .fontWeight(.semibold)
                            Spacer()
                        }
                    }
                    .disabled(exportFormat == .csv && endDate < startDate)
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle("Export Data")
            // MARK: - Exporters
            .background {
                EmptyView()
                    .fileExporter(
                        isPresented: $showCSVExporter,
                        document: csvDocument,
                        contentType: .commaSeparatedText,
                        defaultFilename: defaultCSVName()
                    ) { result in
                        handleResult(result)
                    }
            }
            .background {
                EmptyView()
                    .fileExporter(
                        isPresented: $showJSONExporter,
                        document: jsonDocument,
                        contentType: .json,
                        defaultFilename: defaultJSONName()
                    ) { result in
                        handleResult(result)
                    }
            }
            .alert("Export Failed", isPresented: $showErrorAlert) {
                Button("OK", role: .cancel) { }
            } message: {
                Text(errorMessage)
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - UI Helpers
    
    private func summaryRow(label: String, value: String) -> some View {
        HStack {
            Text(label)
            Spacer()
            Text(value).foregroundColor(.secondary)
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Logic
    
    private func prepareAndExportCSV() {
        csvDocument = prepareCSVDocument()
        showCSVExporter = true
    }
    
    private func prepareAndExportJSON() {
        // Ensure templates are included in the snapshot
        let doc = prepareJSONDocument()
        do {
            let encoder = JSONEncoder()
            _ = try encoder.encode(doc.savedData)
            jsonDocument = doc
            showJSONExporter = true
        } catch {
            print("Encoding Error: \(error)")
            errorMessage = "Could not prepare backup: \(error.localizedDescription)"
            showErrorAlert = true
        }
    }
    
    private func handleResult(_ result: Result<URL, Error>) {
        switch result {
        case .success(let url):
            print("Exported to \(url)")
            dismiss()
        case .failure(let error):
            print("Export failed: \(error.localizedDescription)")
            errorMessage = error.localizedDescription
            showErrorAlert = true
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Document Builders
    
    private func prepareJSONDocument() -> JSONBackupDocument {
        let snapshot = SavedData(
            accounts: data.accounts,
            accountGroups: data.accountGroups,
            categories: data.categories,
            tags: data.tags,
            transactions: data.transactions,
            templates: data.templates, // ENSURED: Templates are passed here
            reminderTime: data.reminderTime,
            insightPresets: data.insightPresets
        )
        return JSONBackupDocument(data: snapshot)
    }

    private func prepareCSVDocument() -> CSVDocument {
        let filtered = data.transactions
            .filter { $0.date >= startDate && $0.date <= endDate }
            .sorted(by: { $0.date < $1.date })

        var csv = "ID,Date,Type,Account,Category,Tags,Amount\n"
        let dateFormatter = DateFormatter()
        dateFormatter.dateFormat = "yyyy-MM-dd"
        
        for tx in filtered {
            let accountName = data.accounts.first(where: { $0.id == tx.accountID })?.name ?? "Unknown"
            let categoryName = data.categories.first(where: { $0.id == tx.categoryID })?.name ?? "Unknown"
            let tagsString = tx.tagIDs
                .compactMap { id in data.tags.first(where: { $0.id == id })?.name }
                .joined(separator: "; ")
            
            let dateStr = dateFormatter.string(from: tx.date)
            let amountStr = String(format: "%.2f", tx.amount)
            let safeTags = tagsString.replacingOccurrences(of: ",", with: " ")
            
            csv += "\(tx.id),\(dateStr),\(tx.type.rawValue),\(accountName),\(categoryName),\(safeTags),\(amountStr)\n"
        }
        return CSVDocument(text: csv)
    }

    // MARK: - Safe Filenames

    private func defaultCSVName() -> String {
        let formatter = DateFormatter()
        formatter.dateFormat = "yyyyMMdd"
        return "Transactions_\(formatter.string(from: startDate))_\(formatter.string(from: endDate))"
    }
    
    private func defaultJSONName() -> String {
        let formatter = DateFormatter()
        formatter.dateFormat = "yyyy-MM-dd"
        return "PocketLedger_Backup_\(formatter.string(from: Date()))"
    }
}
