import SwiftUI

/// Transaction row displaying: emoji | category + tags | amount
struct TransactionRow: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    var transaction: Transaction

    var body: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 12) {
            // VISUAL: Category Emoji (larger, prominent)
            Text(categoryEmoji)
                .font(.title2)
                .frame(width: 40, height: 40)
                .background(Color(.secondarySystemBackground))
                .clipShape(Circle())
            
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                // Primary: Tags (or account name if no tags)
                if !transaction.tagIDs.isEmpty {
                    Text(tagsDisplayString)
                        .font(.body)
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                        .lineLimit(1)
                        .truncationMode(.tail)
                } else if let accName = accountName {
                    Text(accName)
                        .font(.body)
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                } else {
                    Text(categoryName)
                        .font(.body)
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                }
                
                // Secondary: Account Type Icon + Account Name + Link Indicator
                HStack(spacing: 4) {
                    if let account = account {
                        AccountTypeIcon(type: account.accountType, size: 14)
                        Text(account.name)
                    }
                    
                    if transaction.linkedTransactionID != nil {
                        Image(systemName: "link")
                            .font(.caption2)
                    }
                }
                .font(.caption)
                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
            }
            
            Spacer()
            
            // Amount with Sign (monospaced for alignment)
            HStack(spacing: 2) {
                Text(transaction.type == .inflow ? "+" : "-")
                    .foregroundColor(amountColor)
                
                Text(transaction.amount.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                    .foregroundColor(amountColor)
            }
            .font(.body.monospacedDigit())
        }
        .padding(.vertical, 4)
    }

    // MARK: - Helpers
    
    private var account: Account? {
        data.accounts.first(where: { $0.id == transaction.accountID })
    }
    
    private var category: Category? {
        data.categories.first(where: { $0.id == transaction.categoryID })
    }

    private var categoryName: String {
        category?.name ?? "Uncategorized"
    }
    
    private var categoryEmoji: String {
        category?.emoji ?? "📝"
    }
    
    private var accountName: String? {
        account?.name
    }
    
    /// Builds a comma-separated string of tag names
    private var tagsDisplayString: String {
        let tagNames = transaction.tagIDs.compactMap { tagID in
            data.tags.first(where: { $0.id == tagID })?.name
        }
        return tagNames.joined(separator: ", ")
    }
    
    // Logic to distinguish Transfers visually
    private var amountColor: Color {
        if categoryName == "Transfer" { return .blue }
        return transaction.type == .inflow ? .green : .red
    }
}

import SwiftUI

struct AddTransactionView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss
    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    
    // Mode
    let initialType: TransactionType?
    let template: TransactionTemplate?
    let editingTransaction: Transaction?
    // NEW: We hold the linked transaction if we are editing a transfer
    @State private var linkedTransaction: Transaction?

    // MARK: - Form State
    @State private var type: TransactionType
    
    // Transaction Data
    @State private var accountID: UUID?
    @State private var transferTargetAccountID: UUID?
    @State private var categoryID: UUID?
    @State private var selectedTagIDs: Set<UUID> = []
    @State private var amountString: String = ""
    @State private var date: Date = Date()
    @State private var isOutlier: Bool = false
    
    // Template State
    @State private var isShowingTemplateSheet: Bool = false
    @State private var templateName: String = ""

    // Sheet State (Half-Menu Triggers)
    @State private var showAccountPicker = false
    @State private var showTransferTargetPicker = false
    @State private var showCategoryPicker = false
    @State private var showTagPicker = false

    // MARK: - Focus State
    enum Field: Hashable {
        case amount
    }
    
    @FocusState private var focusedField: Field?

    // MARK: - Initialiser
    init(initialType: TransactionType? = nil,
         template: TransactionTemplate? = nil,
         editingTransaction: Transaction? = nil) {
        
        self.initialType = initialType
        self.template = template
        self.editingTransaction = editingTransaction
        
        // 1. Determine Initial Type
        var startType: TransactionType = .outflow
        if let edit = editingTransaction {
            startType = edit.type
        } else if let tpl = template {
            startType = tpl.type
        } else if let t = initialType {
            startType = t
        }
        _type = State(initialValue: startType)

        // 2. Pre-fill Data
        if let edit = editingTransaction {
            _accountID = State(initialValue: edit.accountID)
            _categoryID = State(initialValue: edit.categoryID)
            _selectedTagIDs = State(initialValue: Set(edit.tagIDs))
            _amountString = State(initialValue: String(format: "%.2f", edit.amount))
            _date = State(initialValue: edit.date)
            _isOutlier = State(initialValue: edit.isOutlier)
            // Note: Transfer target is loaded in onAppear via findLinkedTransaction
        } else if let tpl = template {
            _accountID = State(initialValue: tpl.accountID)
            _categoryID = State(initialValue: tpl.categoryID)
            _selectedTagIDs = State(initialValue: Set(tpl.tagIDs))
            _isOutlier = State(initialValue: tpl.isOutlier)
            _transferTargetAccountID = State(initialValue: tpl.transferTargetAccountID)
        }
    }

    // MARK: - Body

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                // SECTION 1: AMOUNT
                Section(header: Text("Amount")) {
                    TextField("0.00", text: $amountString)
                        .keyboardType(.numbersAndPunctuation)
                        .font(.system(size: 44, weight: .bold))
                        .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                        .focused($focusedField, equals: .amount)
                        .foregroundColor(amountColor)
                        .onSubmit {
                            handleAmountSubmit()
                        }
                }
                .listRowBackground(Color.clear)

                // SECTION 2: TYPE
                Section {
                    if editingTransaction == nil {
                        Picker("Type", selection: $type) {
                            ForEach(TransactionType.allCases) { t in
                                Text(t.rawValue.capitalized).tag(t)
                            }
                        }
                        .pickerStyle(SegmentedPickerStyle())
                    } else if let _ = linkedTransaction {
                        // If editing a linked transfer, show a locked label
                        HStack {
                            Text("Transaction Type")
                            Spacer()
                            HStack {
                                Image(systemName: "link")
                                Text("Linked Transfer")
                            }
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        }
                    }
                    
                    // ACCOUNT SELECTION
                    if type == .transfer {
                        Button {
                            showAccountPicker = true
                        } label: {
                            HStack {
                                Text("From Account")
                                Spacer()
                                Text(accountName(for: accountID) ?? "Select")
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            }
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                        
                        Button {
                            showTransferTargetPicker = true
                        } label: {
                            HStack {
                                Text("To Account")
                                Spacer()
                                Text(accountName(for: transferTargetAccountID) ?? "Select")
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            }
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                    } else {
                        Button {
                            showAccountPicker = true
                        } label: {
                            HStack {
                                Text("Account")
                                Spacer()
                                Text(accountName(for: accountID) ?? "Select")
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            }
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                    }
                }

                // SECTION 3: CATEGORY
                if type != .transfer {
                    Section {
                        Button {
                            showCategoryPicker = true
                        } label: {
                            HStack {
                                Text("Category")
                                Spacer()
                                Text(categoryName(for: categoryID) ?? "Select")
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            }
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                    }
                }

                // SECTION 4: TAGS
                Section {
                    Button {
                        showTagPicker = true
                    } label: {
                        HStack {
                            Text("Tags")
                            Spacer()
                            if selectedTagIDs.isEmpty {
                                Text("None").foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            } else {
                                Text("\(selectedTagIDs.count) selected")
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    .foregroundColor(.primary)
                }

                // SECTION 5: DATE
                Section {
                    HStack(spacing: 12) {
                        Button {
                            date = Calendar.current.date(byAdding: .day, value: -1, to: Date()) ?? Date()
                        } label: {
                            Text("Yesterday")
                                .font(.subheadline)
                                .fontWeight(.medium)
                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                .padding(.vertical, 8)
                                .background(Color(.secondarySystemBackground))
                                .cornerRadius(8)
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        
                        Button {
                            date = Date()
                        } label: {
                            Text("Today")
                                .font(.subheadline)
                                .fontWeight(.medium)
                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                .padding(.vertical, 8)
                                .background(Color(.secondarySystemBackground))
                                .cornerRadius(8)
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                    }
                    .listRowInsets(EdgeInsets(top: 10, leading: 20, bottom: 0, trailing: 20))
                    .listRowBackground(Color.clear)
                    
                    DatePicker("Date", selection: $date, displayedComponents: [.date])
                }

                // SECTION 6: OPTIONS
                Section(header: Text("Options")) {
                    Toggle("Flag as Outlier", isOn: $isOutlier)
                    
                    if editingTransaction == nil {
                        Button {
                            templateName = ""
                            isShowingTemplateSheet = true
                        } label: {
                            Text("Save as Template")
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            .formToolbar(
                title: navTitle,
                canSave: canSave,
                onSave: { saveTransaction(shouldDismiss: true) },
                onCancel: { dismiss() },
                onSaveAndNew: editingTransaction == nil ? { saveAndAddAnother() } : nil
            )
            .alert("Save Template", isPresented: $isShowingTemplateSheet) {
                TextField("Template Name", text: $templateName)
                Button("Save") { saveTemplate() }
                Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) { }
            } message: {
                Text("Enter a name for this template.")
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: $showAccountPicker) {
                advanceAfterAccountSelection()
            } content: {
                NavigationStack {
                    // Filter accounts based on transaction type
                    SelectionView(
                        title: type == .transfer ? "From Account" : "Account",
                        items: accountsForSourceSelection,
                        selectedID: $accountID
                    )
                }
                .presentationDetents([.medium, .large])
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: $showTransferTargetPicker) {
                advanceAfterTransferTargetSelection()
            } content: {
                NavigationStack {
                    // All accounts except the source (Debt accounts ARE valid targets)
                    SelectionView(
                        title: "To Account",
                        items: data.accounts.filter { $0.id != accountID && !$0.isArchived },
                        selectedID: $transferTargetAccountID
                    )
                }
                .presentationDetents([.medium, .large])
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: $showCategoryPicker) {
                advanceAfterCategorySelection()
            } content: {
                NavigationStack {
                    SelectionView(title: "Category", items: filteredCategories, selectedID: $categoryID)
                }
                .presentationDetents([.medium, .large])
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: $showTagPicker) {
                NavigationStack {
                    MultiSelectionView(title: "Tags", items: data.tags, selectedIDs: $selectedTagIDs)
                }
                .presentationDetents([.medium, .large])
            }
            .onAppear {
                if amountString.isEmpty && editingTransaction == nil {
                    focusedField = .amount
                }
                // NEW: If editing, try to find the linked transaction to populate "Target Account"
                if let edit = editingTransaction,
                   let linkedID = edit.linkedTransactionID,
                   let partner = data.transactions.first(where: { $0.id == linkedID }) {
                    self.linkedTransaction = partner
                    self.transferTargetAccountID = partner.accountID
                    self.type = .transfer // Ensure UI shows as transfer
                }
            }
            .decimalPadToolbar()
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Account Filtering
    
    /// Accounts available for selection as transaction source.
    /// - Inflow: All non-archived accounts (money can come INTO any account)
    /// - Outflow: Excludes pure Debt accounts (can't spend FROM a mortgage)
    /// - Transfer (From): Excludes pure Debt accounts (can't transfer FROM a mortgage)
    private var accountsForSourceSelection: [Account] {
        let activeAccounts = data.accounts.filter { !$0.isArchived }
        
        switch type {
        case .inflow:
            // Money can flow INTO any account
            return activeAccounts
        case .outflow, .transfer:
            // Can't spend FROM or transfer FROM a pure Debt account
            // (Debt accounts only receive payments, they don't originate spending)
            return activeAccounts.filter { $0.accountType != .debt }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Helpers
    
    private var amountColor: Color {
        switch type {
        case .inflow: return .green
        case .outflow: return .primary
        case .transfer: return .blue
        }
    }
    
    private var navTitle: String {
        if let _ = editingTransaction { return "Edit Transaction" }
        return type == .transfer ? "New Transfer" : "New Transaction"
    }

    // MARK: - Logic
    
    private func saveTemplate() {
        let name = templateName.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !name.isEmpty else { return }
        
        data.addTemplate(
            name: name,
            type: type,
            accountID: accountID,
            categoryID: categoryID,
            tagIDs: Array(selectedTagIDs),
            isOutlier: isOutlier,
            transferTargetAccountID: type == .transfer ? transferTargetAccountID : nil
        )
    }
    
    private var filteredCategories: [Category] {
        data.categories.filter {
            $0.type.rawValue == type.rawValue &&
            $0.name != "Transfer"
        }
    }

    private func accountName(for id: UUID?) -> String? {
        guard let id = id else { return nil }
        return data.accounts.first(where: { $0.id == id })?.name
    }

    private func categoryName(for id: UUID?) -> String? {
        guard let id = id else { return nil }
        return data.categories.first(where: { $0.id == id })?.name
    }
    
    private var canSave: Bool {
        let amount = MathParser.calculate(amountString)
        guard amount > 0 else { return false }
        
        if type == .transfer {
            return accountID != nil && transferTargetAccountID != nil && accountID != transferTargetAccountID
        } else {
            return accountID != nil && categoryID != nil
        }
    }
    
    private enum NavigationStep {
        case account
        case secondary
        case tags
    }
    
    private func checkAndPresentNextField(startingFrom step: NavigationStep) {
        switch step {
        case .account:
            if accountID == nil {
                showAccountPicker = true
                return
            }
            fallthrough
            
        case .secondary:
            if type == .transfer {
                if transferTargetAccountID == nil {
                    showTransferTargetPicker = true
                    return
                }
            } else {
                if categoryID == nil {
                    showCategoryPicker = true
                    return
                }
            }
            fallthrough
            
        case .tags:
            if selectedTagIDs.isEmpty {
                showTagPicker = true
                return
            }
        }
    }
    
    private func handleAmountSubmit() {
        focusedField = nil
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.1) {
            checkAndPresentNextField(startingFrom: .account)
        }
    }
    
    private func advanceAfterAccountSelection() {
        guard accountID != nil else { return }
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.3) {
            checkAndPresentNextField(startingFrom: .secondary)
        }
    }
    
    private func advanceAfterTransferTargetSelection() {
        guard transferTargetAccountID != nil else { return }
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.3) {
            checkAndPresentNextField(startingFrom: .tags)
        }
    }
    
    private func advanceAfterCategorySelection() {
        guard categoryID != nil else { return }
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.3) {
            checkAndPresentNextField(startingFrom: .tags)
        }
    }
    
    private func saveAndAddAnother() {
        saveTransaction(shouldDismiss: false)
        amountString = ""
        selectedTagIDs = []
        isOutlier = false
        focusedField = .amount
        let generator = UINotificationFeedbackGenerator()
        generator.notificationOccurred(.success)
    }

    private func saveTransaction(shouldDismiss: Bool = true) {
        let amount = MathParser.calculate(amountString)
        let tags = Array(selectedTagIDs)

        if type == .transfer {
            guard let fromAcc = accountID, let toAcc = transferTargetAccountID else { return }
            
            let outCatID = ensureTransferCategory(type: .outflow)
            let inCatID = ensureTransferCategory(type: .inflow)
            
            if var existing = editingTransaction, let linkedID = existing.linkedTransactionID {
                // UPDATE EXISTING LINKED PAIR
                
                // 1. Update the transaction we are editing
                existing.accountID = fromAcc
                existing.amount = amount
                existing.date = date
                existing.tagIDs = tags
                existing.isOutlier = isOutlier
                data.updateTransaction(existing)
                
                // 2. Update the linked partner
                if var partner = data.transactions.first(where: { $0.id == linkedID }) {
                    partner.accountID = toAcc
                    partner.amount = amount
                    partner.date = date
                    partner.tagIDs = tags
                    partner.isOutlier = isOutlier
                    data.updateTransaction(partner)
                }
                
            } else {
                // CREATE NEW LINKED PAIR
                let id1 = Transaction.generateUniqueTimestampID()
                let id2 = Transaction.generateUniqueTimestampID() + "-2"
                
                let outflow = Transaction(id: id1, linkedTransactionID: id2, type: .outflow, accountID: fromAcc, categoryID: outCatID, tagIDs: tags, amount: amount, date: date, isOutlier: isOutlier)
                
                let inflow = Transaction(id: id2, linkedTransactionID: id1, type: .inflow, accountID: toAcc, categoryID: inCatID, tagIDs: tags, amount: amount, date: date, isOutlier: isOutlier)
                
                data.transactions.append(outflow)
                data.transactions.append(inflow)
                data.save()
            }
            
        } else {
            // NORMAL TRANSACTION
            guard let accountID = accountID, let categoryID = categoryID else { return }
            
            if let existing = editingTransaction {
                var updated = existing
                updated.type = type
                updated.accountID = accountID
                updated.categoryID = categoryID
                updated.tagIDs = tags
                updated.amount = amount
                updated.date = date
                updated.isOutlier = isOutlier
                data.updateTransaction(updated)
            } else {
                data.addTransaction(type: type, accountID: accountID, categoryID: categoryID, tagIDs: tags, amount: amount, date: date, isOutlier: isOutlier)
            }
        }
        
        if shouldDismiss {
            dismiss()
        }
    }
    
    private func ensureTransferCategory(type: CategoryType) -> UUID {
        if let existing = data.categories.first(where: { $0.name == "Transfer" && $0.type == type }) {
            return existing.id
        }
        let newCat = Category(name: "Transfer", type: type, emoji: "⇆")
        data.categories.append(newCat)
        data.save()
        return newCat.id
    }
}


==================================================
FILE: Pocket Ledger/Views/Transactions/TransactionDetailView.swift
==================================================

import SwiftUI

struct TransactionDetailView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    var transaction: Transaction

    var body: some View {
        List {
            // Header Section: Amount and Date
            Section {
                VStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 8) {
                    // Badge
                    Text(displayType)
                        .font(.caption)
                        .fontWeight(.bold)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                        .padding(.vertical, 4)
                        .background(displayColor.opacity(0.1))
                        .foregroundColor(displayColor)
                        .cornerRadius(8)
                    
                    // Amount (NEW: Formatted)
                    Text(transaction.amount.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                        .font(.system(size: 42, weight: .bold, design: .rounded))
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                    
                    Text(transaction.date.formatted(date: .long, time: .shortened))
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                }
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                .padding(.vertical)
            }
            .listRowBackground(Color.clear)

            // Core Details
            Section(header: Text("Details")) {
                DetailRow(label: "Account", value: accountName)
                DetailRow(label: "Category", value: categoryName)
                
                if transaction.isOutlier {
                    HStack {
                        Text("Outlier")
                        Spacer()
                        Image(systemName: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill")
                            .foregroundColor(.orange)
                    }
                }
            }
            
            // Tags Section
            if !transaction.tagIDs.isEmpty {
                Section(header: Text("Tags")) {
                    ForEach(transaction.tagIDs, id: \.self) { tagID in
                        if let tag = data.tags.first(where: { $0.id == tagID }) {
                            HStack {
                                Image(systemName: "tag.fill")
                                    .font(.caption)
                                    .foregroundColor(.blue)
                                Text(tag.name)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            
            // Metadata
            Section(header: Text("Metadata")) {
                DetailRow(label: "ID", value: transaction.id)
                    .font(.caption)
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
            }
        }
        .navigationTitle("Details")
        .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
    }

    // MARK: - Helpers
    
    private var accountName: String {
        data.accounts.first(where: { $0.id == transaction.accountID })?.name ?? "Unknown"
    }
    
    private var categoryName: String {
        data.categories.first(where: { $0.id == transaction.categoryID })?.name ?? "Unknown"
    }
    
    // Display Logic for Transfers
    private var isTransfer: Bool { categoryName == "Transfer" }
    
    private var displayType: String {
        if isTransfer { return "Transfer" }
        return transaction.type == .inflow ? "Inflow" : "Outflow"
    }
    
    private var displayColor: Color {
        if isTransfer { return .blue }
        return transaction.type == .inflow ? .green : .red
    }
}

/// A simple helper row for key-value pairs
struct DetailRow: View {
    let label: String
    let value: String
    
    var body: some View {
        HStack {
            Text(label)
                .foregroundColor(.primary)
            Spacer()
            Text(value)
                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
        }
    }
}

import SwiftUI

struct TransactionListView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData

    @State private var isPresentingBatchTagSheet = false
    @State private var batchTagsToAdd: Set<UUID> = []
    
    // MARK: - Temporal Grouping State
    @State private var collapsedGroups: Set<TimeGroupID> = []
    
    // MARK: - Archive State
    @AppStorage("showArchivedTransactions") private var showArchivedTransactions: Bool = false
    
    // MARK: - Search State
    @State private var searchText: String = ""
    @State private var isSearchActive: Bool = false
    @FocusState private var isSearchFocused: Bool

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            ZStack(alignment: .bottomTrailing) {
                // Main List
                transactionList
                
                // Floating Add Button (only when not in edit mode and not searching)

                    floatingAddButton
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle("Transactions")
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .navigationBarLeading) {
                    Menu {
                        Button {
                            toggleAllGroups()
                        } label: {
                            let allCollapsed = collapsedGroups.count == visibleTimeGroups.count
                            Label(allCollapsed ? "Expand All" : "Collapse All",
                                  systemImage: allCollapsed ? "arrow.down.right.and.arrow.up.left" : "arrow.up.left.and.arrow.down.right")
                        }
                        
                        Divider()
                        
                        Button {
                            showArchivedTransactions.toggle()
                        } label: {
                            Label(showArchivedTransactions ? "Hide Archived" : "Show Archived",
                                  systemImage: showArchivedTransactions ? "eye.slash" : "eye")
                        }
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "ellipsis.circle")
                    }
                }
                
                ToolbarItem(placement: .navigationBarTrailing) {
                    HStack(spacing: 16) {
                        // Search toggle button
                        Button {
                            withAnimation {
                                isSearchActive.toggle()
                                if isSearchActive {
                                    isSearchFocused = true
                                } else {
                                    searchText = ""
                                    isSearchFocused = false
                                }
                            }
                        } label: {
                            Image(systemName: isSearchActive ? "xmark" : "magnifyingglass")
                        }
                        
                        editButton
                    }
                }
            }
            .safeAreaInset(edge: .bottom) {
                if isEditMode && !selection.isEmpty {
                    multiSelectActionBar
                } else if isSearchActive {
                    searchBar
                }
            }
            // Sheets
            .sheet(isPresented: $globalAddTransactionTrigger) {
                AddTransactionView()
                    .environmentObject(data)
            }
            .sheet(item: $transactionToEdit) { transaction in
                AddTransactionView(editingTransaction: transaction)
                    .environmentObject(data)
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: $isPresentingBatchTagSheet) {
                batchTagSheet
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Search Bar
    
    private var searchBar: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 0) {
            Divider()
            
            HStack(spacing: 12) {
                HStack {
                    Image(systemName: "magnifyingglass")
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    
                    TextField("Search tags, categories, accounts, amounts...", text: $searchText)
                        .focused($isSearchFocused)
                        .submitLabel(.search)
                        .autocorrectionDisabled()
                    
                    if !searchText.isEmpty {
                        Button {
                            searchText = ""
                        } label: {
                            Image(systemName: "xmark.circle.fill")
                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        }
                    }
                }
                .padding(10)
                .background(Color(.secondarySystemBackground))
                .cornerRadius(10)
                
                Button("Cancel") {
                    withAnimation {
                        searchText = ""
                        isSearchActive = false
                        isSearchFocused = false
                    }

                .foregroundColor(.blue)
            }
            .padding(.horizontal)
            .padding(.vertical, 10)
            .background(Color(UIColor.systemBackground))
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Main List
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private var transactionList: some View {
        List(selection: $selection) {
            if data.transactions.isEmpty {
                emptyState
            } else if filteredTransactions.isEmpty {
                if isSearchActive && !searchText.isEmpty {
                    noSearchResultsState
                } else if !showArchivedTransactions && visibleTransactions.isEmpty {
                    archivedOnlyState
                } else {
                    emptyState
                }
            } else {
                ForEach(visibleTimeGroups, id: \.id) { group in
                    temporalSection(for: group)
                }
                
                // Pagination: Load Archived Button
                if hasArchivedTransactions && !showArchivedTransactions && !isSearchActive {
                    loadArchivedSection
                }
            }
            
            // Spacer for FAB/action bar/search bar
            Color.clear.frame(height: 100).listRowBackground(Color.clear)
        }
        .environment(\.editMode, .constant(isEditMode ? .active : .inactive))
    }

    private var emptyState: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 12) {
            Image(systemName: "list.bullet.rectangle")
                .font(.system(size: 48))

                .font(.headline)

        .padding(.vertical, 40)

    }
    
    @ViewBuilder

            Image(systemName: "archivebox")
                .font(.system(size: 48))

                .font(.headline)

                .font(.subheadline)

    }
    
    // MARK: - Temporal Section
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private func temporalSection(for group: TimeGroup) -> some View {
        let isCollapsed = collapsedGroups.contains(group.id)
        
        DisclosureGroup(
            isExpanded: Binding(
                get: { !isCollapsed },
                set: { isExpanded in
                    if isExpanded {
                        collapsedGroups.remove(group.id)
                    } else {
                        collapsedGroups.insert(group.id)
                    }
                }
            ),
            content: {
                ForEach(group.transactions) { transaction in
                    if isEditMode {
                        editModeRow(for: transaction)
                    } else {
                        normalModeRow(for: transaction)
                    }
                }
            },
            label: {
                sectionHeader(for: group)
            }
        )
    }
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private func sectionHeader(for group: TimeGroup) -> some View {
        HStack {
            HStack(spacing: 6) {
                if group.period == .archived {
                    Image(systemName: "archivebox.fill")
                        .font(.caption)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                }
                Text(group.displayName)
                    .font(.headline)
                    .foregroundColor(group.period == .archived ? .secondary : .primary)
            }
            
            Spacer()
            
            // Net flow + count
            HStack(spacing: 8) {
                // Net flow indicator
                let netFlow = group.netFlow
                if netFlow != 0 {
                    Text(netFlow >= 0 ? "+\(netFlow.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))" : netFlow.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                        .font(.caption)
                        .fontWeight(.medium)
                        .monospacedDigit()
                        .foregroundColor(netFlow >= 0 ? .green : .red)
                }
                
                // Transaction count
                Text("\(group.transactions.count)")
                    .font(.caption)
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                    .padding(.vertical, 2)
                    .background(Color(.secondarySystemBackground))
                    .clipShape(Capsule())
            }
        }
    }
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private func editModeRow(for transaction: Transaction) -> some View {
        TransactionRow(transaction: transaction)
            .environmentObject(data)

            .swipeActions(edge: .leading) {

                .environmentObject(data)
        }
        .swipeActions(edge: .leading) {
            Button {
                transactionToEdit = transaction
            } label: {
                Label("Edit", systemImage: "pencil")
            }
            .tint(.blue)
        }
        .swipeActions(edge: .trailing) {
            Button(role: .destructive) {
                data.deleteTransaction(transaction)
            } label: {
                Label("Delete", systemImage: "trash")
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Load Archived Section
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private var loadArchivedSection: some View {
        Section {
            Button {
                showArchivedTransactions = true
            } label: {
                HStack {
                    Spacer()
                    VStack(spacing: 4) {
                        Image(systemName: "archivebox")
                            .font(.title3)
                        Text("Show Archived Transactions")
                            .font(.subheadline)
                    }
                    .foregroundColor(.blue)
                    Spacer()
                }
                .padding(.vertical, 8)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Toolbar Buttons
    
    private var editButton: some View {
        Button(isEditMode ? "Done" : "Edit") {
            withAnimation {
                isEditMode.toggle()
                if !isEditMode {
                    selection.removeAll()
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Multi-Select Action Bar
    
    private var multiSelectActionBar: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 0) {
            Divider()
            
            HStack(spacing: 20) {
                // Selection count
                Text("\(selection.count) selected")
                    .font(.subheadline)
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                
                Spacer()
                
                // Add Tags button
                Button {
                    batchTagsToAdd = []
                    isPresentingBatchTagSheet = true
                } label: {
                    VStack(spacing: 4) {
                        Image(systemName: "tag")
                            .font(.title3)
                        Text("Add Tags")
                            .font(.caption2)
                    }
                }
                .foregroundColor(.blue)
                
                // Delete button
                Button(role: .destructive) {
                    deleteSelectedTransactions()
                } label: {
                    VStack(spacing: 4) {
                        Image(systemName: "trash")
                            .font(.title3)
                        Text("Delete")
                            .font(.caption2)
                    }
                }
                .foregroundColor(.red)
            }
            .padding(.horizontal)
            .padding(.vertical, 12)
            .background(Color(UIColor.systemBackground))
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Floating Add Button
    
    private var floatingAddButton: some View {
        Button {
            globalAddTransactionTrigger = true
        } label: {
            Image(systemName: "plus")
                .font(.title2.weight(.bold))
                .foregroundColor(.white)
                .frame(width: 56, height: 56)
                .background(Color.blue)
                .clipShape(Circle())
                .shadow(color: .black.opacity(0.3), radius: 4, x: 0, y: 4)
        }
        .padding()
        .padding(.bottom, 10)
    }
    
    // MARK: - Batch Tag Sheet
    
    private var batchTagSheet: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                BatchTagSelectionContent(
                    selectedIDs: $batchTagsToAdd,
                    onApply: { applyTagsToSelection() }
                )
            }
            .navigationTitle("Add Tags")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .cancellationAction) {
                    Button("Cancel") {
                        isPresentingBatchTagSheet = false
                    }
                }
            }
            .safeAreaInset(edge: .bottom) {
                VStack(spacing: 0) {
                    Divider()
                    
                    Button {
                        applyTagsToSelection()
                    } label: {
                        Text("Apply Tags")
                            .fontWeight(.semibold)
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                            .padding()
                            .background(batchTagsToAdd.isEmpty ? Color.gray : Color.blue)
                            .foregroundColor(.white)
                            .cornerRadius(12)
                    }
                    .disabled(batchTagsToAdd.isEmpty)
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                    .padding(.vertical, 12)
                    .background(Color(.systemBackground))
                }
            }
        }
        .presentationDetents([.medium, .large])
    }
    
    // MARK: - Search Logic
    
    /// Checks if a transaction matches the search query
    private func transactionMatchesSearch(_ transaction: Transaction, query: String) -> Bool {
        let lowercasedQuery = query.lowercased()
        
        // Match against tags
        let tagNames = transaction.tagIDs.compactMap { tagID in
            data.tags.first(where: { $0.id == tagID })?.name.lowercased()
        }
        if tagNames.contains(where: { $0.contains(lowercasedQuery) }) {
            return true
        }
        
        // Match against category
        if let category = data.categories.first(where: { $0.id == transaction.categoryID }) {
            if category.name.lowercased().contains(lowercasedQuery) {
                return true
            }
        }
        
        // Match against account
        if let account = data.accounts.first(where: { $0.id == transaction.accountID }) {
            if account.name.lowercased().contains(lowercasedQuery) {
                return true
            }
        }
        
        // Match against amount (as string)
        let amountString = String(format: "%.2f", transaction.amount)
        if amountString.contains(lowercasedQuery) {
            return true
        }
        
        // Match against whole number amount
        let wholeAmount = String(Int(transaction.amount))
        if wholeAmount == lowercasedQuery || amountString.hasPrefix(lowercasedQuery) {
            return true
        }
        
        return false
    }
    
    // MARK: - Temporal Grouping Logic
    
    /// Archive cutoff: 3 months ago
    private var archiveCutoffDate: Date {
        Calendar.current.date(byAdding: .month, value: -3, to: Date()) ?? Date.distantPast
    }
    
    /// All transactions that pass the archive filter (but not search filter)
    private var visibleTransactions: [Transaction] {
        let cutoffDate = archiveCutoffDate
        
        return data.transactions.filter { transaction in
            if showArchivedTransactions {
                return true
            } else {
                return transaction.date >= cutoffDate
            }
        }
    }
    
    /// Transactions filtered by both archive AND search
    private var filteredTransactions: [Transaction] {
        let base = visibleTransactions
        
        guard isSearchActive && !searchText.isEmpty else {
            return base
        }
        
        return base.filter { transactionMatchesSearch($0, query: searchText) }
    }
    
    private var hasArchivedTransactions: Bool {
        data.transactions.contains { $0.date < archiveCutoffDate }
    }
    
    private var visibleTimeGroups: [TimeGroup] {
        // Use filtered transactions (includes search filtering)
        let sorted = filteredTransactions.sorted { $0.date > $1.date }
        
        var groups: [TimeGroup] = []
        let calendar = Calendar.current
        let now = Date()
        
        // Define time boundaries
        let startOfToday = calendar.startOfDay(for: now)
        let startOfYesterday = calendar.date(byAdding: .day, value: -1, to: startOfToday)!
        let startOfThisWeek = calendar.date(byAdding: .day, value: -7, to: startOfToday)!
        let startOfThisMonth = calendar.dateInterval(of: .month, for: now)?.start ?? startOfToday
        
        // Last month boundaries
        let lastMonthDate = calendar.date(byAdding: .month, value: -1, to: now)!
        let startOfLastMonth = calendar.dateInterval(of: .month, for: lastMonthDate)?.start ?? lastMonthDate
        let endOfLastMonth = calendar.dateInterval(of: .month, for: lastMonthDate)?.end ?? lastMonthDate
        
        // Two months ago boundaries
        let twoMonthsAgoDate = calendar.date(byAdding: .month, value: -2, to: now)!
        let startOfTwoMonthsAgo = calendar.dateInterval(of: .month, for: twoMonthsAgoDate)?.start ?? twoMonthsAgoDate
        let endOfTwoMonthsAgo = calendar.dateInterval(of: .month, for: twoMonthsAgoDate)?.end ?? twoMonthsAgoDate
        
        // Group transactions by time period
        var futureTransactions: [Transaction] = []
        var todayTransactions: [Transaction] = []
        var yesterdayTransactions: [Transaction] = []
        var thisWeekTransactions: [Transaction] = []  // 2 days ago to 7 days ago
        var thisMonthTransactions: [Transaction] = [] // After this week, within this month
        var lastMonthTransactions: [Transaction] = []
        var twoMonthsAgoTransactions: [Transaction] = []
        var archivedTransactions: [Transaction] = []
        
        for transaction in sorted {
            let txDate = transaction.date
            
            if txDate > now {
                // Future
                futureTransactions.append(transaction)
            } else if txDate >= startOfToday {
                // Today
                todayTransactions.append(transaction)
            } else if txDate >= startOfYesterday {
                // Yesterday
                yesterdayTransactions.append(transaction)
            } else if txDate >= startOfThisWeek {
                // This Week (2 days ago to 7 days ago)
                thisWeekTransactions.append(transaction)
            } else if txDate >= startOfThisMonth {
                // This Month (after this week but still in current month)
                thisMonthTransactions.append(transaction)
            } else if txDate >= startOfLastMonth && txDate < endOfLastMonth {
                // Last Month
                lastMonthTransactions.append(transaction)
            } else if txDate >= startOfTwoMonthsAgo && txDate < endOfTwoMonthsAgo {
                // Two Months Ago
                twoMonthsAgoTransactions.append(transaction)
            } else {
                // Archived (3+ months old)
                archivedTransactions.append(transaction)
            }
        }
        
        // Build groups in order
        if !futureTransactions.isEmpty {
            groups.append(TimeGroup(period: .future, transactions: futureTransactions))
        }
        if !todayTransactions.isEmpty {
            groups.append(TimeGroup(period: .today, transactions: todayTransactions))
        }
        if !yesterdayTransactions.isEmpty {
            groups.append(TimeGroup(period: .yesterday, transactions: yesterdayTransactions))
        }
        if !thisWeekTransactions.isEmpty {
            groups.append(TimeGroup(period: .thisWeek, transactions: thisWeekTransactions))
        }
        if !thisMonthTransactions.isEmpty {
            groups.append(TimeGroup(period: .thisMonth, transactions: thisMonthTransactions))
        }
        if !lastMonthTransactions.isEmpty {
            groups.append(TimeGroup(period: .lastMonth(lastMonthDate), transactions: lastMonthTransactions))
        }
        if !twoMonthsAgoTransactions.isEmpty {
            groups.append(TimeGroup(period: .twoMonthsAgo(twoMonthsAgoDate), transactions: twoMonthsAgoTransactions))
        }
        
        // Add archived group if showing archived
        if showArchivedTransactions && !archivedTransactions.isEmpty {
            groups.append(TimeGroup(period: .archived, transactions: archivedTransactions))
        }
        
        return groups
    }
    
    // MARK: - Actions
    
    private func toggleAllGroups() {
        let allGroupIDs = Set(visibleTimeGroups.map { $0.id })
        let allCollapsed = collapsedGroups.count == allGroupIDs.count
        
        withAnimation {
            if allCollapsed {
                collapsedGroups.removeAll()
            } else {
                collapsedGroups = allGroupIDs
            }
        }
    }

    private func deleteSelectedTransactions() {
        withAnimation {
            // Include linked transactions in selection
            var idsToDelete = selection
            
            for id in selection {
                if let tx = data.transactions.first(where: { $0.id == id }),
                   let linkedID = tx.linkedTransactionID,
                   let linkedTx = data.transactions.first(where: { $0.id == linkedID }) {
                    idsToDelete.insert(linkedTx.id)
                }
            }
            
            data.transactions.removeAll { transaction in
                idsToDelete.contains(transaction.id)
            }
            
            data.save()
            selection.removeAll()
            isEditMode = false
        }
    }

    private func applyTagsToSelection() {
        guard !batchTagsToAdd.isEmpty else { return }
        
        for id in selection {
            if let index = data.transactions.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) {
                var transaction = data.transactions[index]
                
                // Merge existing tags with new tags
                let existingTags = Set(transaction.tagIDs)
                let mergedTags = existingTags.union(batchTagsToAdd)
                
                transaction.tagIDs = Array(mergedTags)
                data.transactions[index] = transaction
            }
        }
        
        data.save()
        
        // Reset state
        batchTagsToAdd = []
        isPresentingBatchTagSheet = false
        selection = []
        isEditMode = false
        
        // Haptic feedback
        let generator = UINotificationFeedbackGenerator()
        generator.notificationOccurred(.success)
    }
}

// MARK: - Supporting Types

extension TransactionListView {
    enum TimePeriod: Hashable {
        case future
        case today
        case yesterday
        case thisWeek           // 2 days ago through 7 days ago
        case thisMonth          // Earlier this month (after this week)
        case lastMonth(Date)    // Previous calendar month
        case twoMonthsAgo(Date) // Two calendar months ago
        case archived           // 3+ months old
        
        var displayName: String {
            let monthFormatter = DateFormatter()
            monthFormatter.dateFormat = "MMMM"
            
            switch self {
            case .future:
                return "Upcoming"
            case .today:
                return "Today"
            case .yesterday:
                return "Yesterday"
            case .thisWeek:
                return "This Week"
            case .thisMonth:
                return "This Month"
            case .lastMonth(let date):
                return monthFormatter.string(from: date)
            case .twoMonthsAgo(let date):
                return monthFormatter.string(from: date)
            case .archived:
                return "Archived"
            }
        }
        
        var autoExpand: Bool {
            switch self {
            case .future, .today, .yesterday, .thisWeek:
                return true
            default:
                return false
            }
        }
    }
    
    struct TimeGroup: Identifiable {
        let id: TimeGroupID
        let period: TimePeriod
        let transactions: [Transaction]
        
        var displayName: String {
            period.displayName
        }
        
        /// Net flow: inflows - outflows (transfers excluded)
        var netFlow: Double {
            var total: Double = 0
            for tx in transactions {
                switch tx.type {
                case .inflow:
                    total += tx.amount
                case .outflow:
                    total -= tx.amount
                case .transfer:
                    // Transfers are net-zero, skip
                    break
                }
            }
            return total
        }
        
        init(period: TimePeriod, transactions: [Transaction]) {
            self.period = period
            self.transactions = transactions
            
            // Generate unique ID based on period
            switch period {
            case .future:
                self.id = TimeGroupID(value: "future")
            case .today:
                self.id = TimeGroupID(value: "today")
            case .yesterday:
                self.id = TimeGroupID(value: "yesterday")
            case .thisWeek:
                self.id = TimeGroupID(value: "thisWeek")
            case .thisMonth:
                self.id = TimeGroupID(value: "thisMonth")
            case .lastMonth(let date):
                let formatter = DateFormatter()
                formatter.dateFormat = "yyyy-MM"
                self.id = TimeGroupID(value: "lastMonth-\(formatter.string(from: date))")
            case .twoMonthsAgo(let date):
                let formatter = DateFormatter()
                formatter.dateFormat = "yyyy-MM"
                self.id = TimeGroupID(value: "twoMonthsAgo-\(formatter.string(from: date))")
            case .archived:
                self.id = TimeGroupID(value: "archived")
            }
        }
    }
    
    struct TimeGroupID: Hashable {
        let value: String
    }
}

// MARK: - Batch Tag Selection Content

struct BatchTagSelectionContent: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @Binding var selectedIDs: Set<UUID>
    let onApply: () -> Void
    
    @State private var searchText = ""
    @FocusState private var isSearchFocused: Bool
    
    var filteredItems: [Tag] {
        let list = searchText.isEmpty
            ? data.tags
            : data.tags.filter { $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(searchText) }
        return list.sorted { $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveCompare($1.name) == .orderedAscending }
    }
    
    var showCreationRow: Bool {
        !searchText.isEmpty && !data.tags.contains(where: { $0.name.caseInsensitiveCompare(searchText) == .orderedSame })
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 0) {
            // Search Header
            HStack {
                Image(systemName: "tag")
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                TextField("Find or create tags...", text: $searchText)
                    .focused($isSearchFocused)
                    .submitLabel(.done)
                    .onSubmit {
                        handleKeyboardSubmit()
                    }
                if !searchText.isEmpty {
                    Button {
                        searchText = ""
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "xmark.circle.fill")
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                }
            }
            .padding(8)
            .background(Color(.secondarySystemBackground))
            .cornerRadius(10)
            .padding()
            
            // Tag List
            List {
                if showCreationRow {
                    Button(action: createAndSelectTag) {
                        HStack {
                            Image(systemName: "plus.circle.fill")
                                .foregroundColor(.green)
                            Text("Create \"\(searchText)\"")
                                .fontWeight(.medium)
                        }
                    }
                }

                ForEach(filteredItems) { item in
                    Button {
                        toggleSelection(item)
                    } label: {
                        HStack {
                            Text(item.name)
                            Spacer()
                            if selectedIDs.contains(item.id) {
                                Image(systemName: "checkmark.circle.fill")
                                    .foregroundColor(.blue)
                                    .fontWeight(.bold)
                            } else {
                                Image(systemName: "circle")
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary.opacity(0.3))
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    .foregroundColor(.primary)
                }
                
                // Spacer for bottom toolbar
                Color.clear.frame(height: 80).listRowBackground(Color.clear)
            }
        }
        .onAppear {
            DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.1) {
                isSearchFocused = true
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Helpers
    
    private func handleKeyboardSubmit() {
        if searchText.isEmpty {
            if !selectedIDs.isEmpty {
                onApply()
            }
        } else if filteredItems.count == 1 {
            toggleSelection(filteredItems[0])
            searchText = ""
            isSearchFocused = true
        } else if showCreationRow {
            createAndSelectTag()
        }
    }
    
    private func toggleSelection(_ item: Tag) {
        if selectedIDs.contains(item.id) {
            selectedIDs.remove(item.id)
        } else {
            selectedIDs.insert(item.id)
        }
    }
    
    private func createAndSelectTag() {
        let cleanName = searchText.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        guard !cleanName.isEmpty else { return }
        
        data.addTag(name: cleanName)
        
        if let newTag = data.tags.first(where: { $0.name == cleanName }) {
            selectedIDs.insert(newTag.id)
        }
        
        searchText = ""
        isSearchFocused = true
    }
}
