import SwiftUI

/// Displays details about an account including its transactions and statistics.
struct AccountDetailView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    
    // The account passed in is just used for its ID to find the "Live" version
    let account: Account

    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    @AppStorage("fiscalStartDay") private var fiscalStartDay: Int = 1
    
    /// Fetches the latest version of the account from the source of truth.
    /// This ensures that when AppData changes (e.g. archiving), this view updates immediately.
    private var liveAccount: Account {
        data.accounts.first(where: { $0.id == account.id }) ?? account
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        List {
            // SECTION 1: Balance Card (Type-Specific)
            balanceSection
            
            // SECTION 2: Target Progress (Goals, Budgets, Debts)
            if shouldShowTargetProgress {
                targetProgressSection
            }
            
            // SECTION 3: Installment Calculator (Goals & Debts with target dates)
            if shouldShowInstallmentCalculator {
                installmentCalculatorSection
            }
            
            // SECTION 4: Statistics
            statisticsSection
            
            // SECTION 5: Recent Transactions
            recentTransactionsSection
            
            // SECTION 6: Archive Controls
            archiveSection
        }
        .navigationTitle(liveAccount.name)
        .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
    }
    
    // MARK: - Balance Section
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private var balanceSection: some View {
        Section {
            VStack(spacing: 12) {
                // Account Type Badge
                HStack {
                    Image(systemName: liveAccount.accountType.icon)
                        .foregroundColor(accountTypeColor)
                    Text(liveAccount.accountType.rawValue + " Account")
                        .font(.subheadline)
                        .foregroundColor(accountTypeColor)
                    Spacer()
                }
                
                // Current Balance
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                    Text(balanceLabel)
                        .font(.caption)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    
                    Text(data.balance(for: liveAccount).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                        .font(.system(size: 36, weight: .bold, design: .rounded))
                        .foregroundColor(balanceColor)
                }
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                
                // Context-specific subtitle
                if let subtitle = balanceSubtitle {
                    Text(subtitle)
                        .font(.caption)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                }
            }
            .padding(.vertical, 8)
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Target Progress Section
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private var targetProgressSection: some View {
        if let target = liveAccount.targetAmount, target > 0 {
            Section(header: Text(progressSectionTitle)) {
                VStack(spacing: 16) {
                    // Progress Bar
                    progressBar(current: data.balance(for: liveAccount), target: target)
                    
                    // Progress Stats
                    HStack {
                        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                            Text(currentAmountLabel)
                                .font(.caption)
                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            Text(data.balance(for: liveAccount).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                                .font(.headline)
                        }
                        
                        Spacer()
                        
                        VStack(alignment: .trailing, spacing: 4) {
                            Text(targetAmountLabel)
                                .font(.caption)
                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            Text(target.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                                .font(.headline)
                        }
                    }
                    
                    // Status Badge
                    statusBadge(current: data.balance(for: liveAccount), target: target)
                }
                .padding(.vertical, 8)
            }
        } else if liveAccount.accountType == .debt {
            // Debt account with target = 0 or nil
            Section(header: Text("Debt Tracking")) {
                VStack(spacing: 12) {
                    HStack {
                        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                            Text("Current Debt")
                                .font(.caption)
                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            Text(data.balance(for: liveAccount).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                                .font(.headline)
                        }
                        
                        Spacer()
                        
                        VStack(alignment: .trailing, spacing: 4) {
                            Text("Payoff Goal")
                                .font(.caption)
                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            Text("$0.00")
                                .font(.headline)
                        }
                    }
                    
                    if data.balance(for: liveAccount) > 0 {
                        HStack {
                            Image(systemName: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill")
                                .foregroundColor(.orange)
                            Text("Set a target date to calculate payment plan")
                                .font(.caption)
                        }
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                        .padding(8)
                        .background(Color.orange.opacity(0.1))
                        .cornerRadius(8)
                    } else {
                        HStack {
                            Image(systemName: "checkmark.circle.fill")
                                .foregroundColor(.green)
                            Text("Debt paid off! 🎉")
                                .font(.subheadline)
                                .fontWeight(.medium)
                        }
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                        .padding(8)
                        .background(Color.green.opacity(0.1))
                        .cornerRadius(8)
                    }
                }
                .padding(.vertical, 8)
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Installment Calculator Section
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private var installmentCalculatorSection: some View {
        if let targetDate = liveAccount.targetDate,
           let frequency = liveAccount.paymentFrequency {
            
            Section(header: Text(liveAccount.accountType == .goal ? "Savings Plan" : "Payment Plan")) {
                VStack(spacing: 12) {
                    // Target Date
                    HStack {
                        Text("Target Date")
                        Spacer()
                        Text(targetDate, style: .date)
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                    
                    Divider()
                    
                    // Frequency
                    HStack {
                        Text("Payment Frequency")
                        Spacer()
                        Text(frequency.rawValue)
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                    
                    Divider()
                    
                    // Calculations
                    if let installment = calculateInstallment() {
                        VStack(spacing: 8) {
                            HStack {
                                Text("Payments Remaining")
                                Spacer()
                                Text("\(installment.periodsRemaining)")
                                    .fontWeight(.semibold)
                            }
                            
                            HStack {
                                Text("Required per \(frequency.rawValue.lowercased())")
                                Spacer()
                                Text(installment.amountPerPeriod.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                                    .font(.headline)
                                    .foregroundColor(liveAccount.accountType == .goal ? .blue : .orange)
                            }
                        }
                        .padding(.top, 4)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Statistics Section
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private var statisticsSection: some View {
        Section(header: Text("Statistics"), footer: fiscalRangeFooter) {
            let monthlyTotals = calculateCurrentMonthTotals()
            let stats = data.stats(for: liveAccount)
            
            // This Period Stats
            switch liveAccount.accountType {
            case .debit, .goal:
                StatRow(title: "Income (This Month)",
                       value: monthlyTotals.inflow,
                       symbol: currencySymbol,
                       color: .green)
                StatRow(title: "Expenses (This Month)",
                       value: monthlyTotals.outflow,
                       symbol: currencySymbol,
                       color: .red)
                
            case .budget:
                StatRow(title: "Spent (This Month)",
                       value: monthlyTotals.outflow,
                       symbol: currencySymbol,
                       color: .red)
                if let target = liveAccount.targetAmount {
                    StatRow(title: "Budget",
                           value: target,
                           symbol: currencySymbol,
                           color: .purple)
                }
                
            case .debt:
                StatRow(title: "Payments Made (This Month)",
                       value: monthlyTotals.inflow,
                       symbol: currencySymbol,
                       color: .green)
                StatRow(title: "New Charges (This Month)",
                       value: monthlyTotals.outflow,
                       symbol: currencySymbol,
                       color: .red)
            }
            
            Divider()
            
            // Average Stats
            switch liveAccount.accountType {
            case .debit, .goal:
                StatRow(title: "Avg Income / Month",
                       value: stats.monthlyInflow,
                       symbol: currencySymbol)
                StatRow(title: "Avg Expenses / Month",
                       value: stats.monthlyOutflow,
                       symbol: currencySymbol)
                
            case .budget:
                StatRow(title: "Avg Spending / Month",
                       value: stats.monthlyOutflow,
                       symbol: currencySymbol)
                
            case .debt:
                StatRow(title: "Avg Payment / Month",
                       value: stats.monthlyInflow,
                       symbol: currencySymbol)
                StatRow(title: "Avg New Charges / Month",
                       value: stats.monthlyOutflow,
                       symbol: currencySymbol)
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Recent Transactions Section
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private var recentTransactionsSection: some View {
        Section(header: Text("Recent Transactions (Last 20)")) {
            let recentTransactions = data.transactions(for: liveAccount)
                .sorted { $0.date > $1.date }
                .prefix(20)
            
            if recentTransactions.isEmpty {
                Text("No transactions yet")
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
            } else {
                ForEach(recentTransactions) { transaction in
                    NavigationLink(destination: TransactionDetailView(transaction: transaction)) {
                        TransactionRow(transaction: transaction)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Archive Section
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private var archiveSection: some View {
        Section(footer: archiveFooter) {
            if liveAccount.isArchived {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
                    HStack {
                        Image(systemName: "archivebox.fill")
                            .foregroundColor(.orange)
                        Text("This account is archived")
                            .font(.subheadline)
                            .fontWeight(.semibold)
                    }
                    
                    if let archivedDate = liveAccount.archivedDate {
                        Text("Archived on \(archivedDate.formatted(date: .long, time: .omitted))")
                            .font(.caption)
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                    
                    Text("Archived accounts are hidden from the main accounts list but their transactions are still included in Insights.")
                        .font(.caption)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    
                    Button {
                        // Action wrapped in animation for instant visual feedback
                        withAnimation {
                            data.unarchiveAccount(liveAccount)
                        }
                    } label: {
                        HStack {
                            Image(systemName: "arrow.uturn.backward.circle.fill")
                            Text("Unarchive Account")
                        }
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                        .padding()
                        .background(Color.blue)
                        .foregroundColor(.white)
                        .cornerRadius(12)
                    }
                    .padding(.top, 4)
                }
                .padding(.vertical, 8)
            } else {
                Button(role: .destructive) {
                    // Action wrapped in animation for instant visual feedback
                    withAnimation {
                        data.archiveAccount(liveAccount)
                    }
                } label: {
                    HStack {
                        Image(systemName: "archivebox")
                        Text("Archive Account")
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    private var archiveFooter: Text? {
        if !liveAccount.isArchived {
            return Text("Archive this account to hide it from the main list. You can unarchive it anytime, and all transaction history will be preserved.")
        }
        return nil
    }
    
    // MARK: - Helper Views
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private func progressBar(current: Double, target: Double) -> some View {
        GeometryReader { geometry in
            ZStack(alignment: .leading) {
                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8)
                    .fill(Color.gray.opacity(0.2))
                    .frame(height: 12)
                
                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8)
                    .fill(progressBarColor(current: current, target: target))
                    .frame(width: progressBarWidth(current: current, target: target, totalWidth: geometry.size.width),
                           height: 12)
            }
        }
        .frame(height: 12)
    }
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private func statusBadge(current: Double, target: Double) -> some View {
        switch liveAccount.accountType {
        case .budget:
            let remaining = target - current
            HStack {
                Image(systemName: remaining >= 0 ? "checkmark.circle.fill" : "exclamationmark.triangle.fill")
                    .foregroundColor(remaining >= 0 ? .green : .red)
                if remaining >= 0 {
                    Text("\(remaining.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol)) remaining (\(Int((current/target)*100))% used)")
                } else {
                    Text("Over budget by \(abs(remaining).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))")
                }
            }
            .font(.subheadline)
            .fontWeight(.medium)
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
            .padding(8)
            .background(remaining >= 0 ? Color.green.opacity(0.1) : Color.red.opacity(0.1))
            .cornerRadius(8)
            
        case .goal:
            if current >= target {
                HStack {
                    Image(systemName: "star.fill")
                        .foregroundColor(.yellow)
                    Text("Goal reached! 🎉")
                }
                .font(.subheadline)
                .fontWeight(.medium)
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                .padding(8)
                .background(Color.green.opacity(0.1))
                .cornerRadius(8)
            } else {
                let remaining = target - current
                HStack {
                    Image(systemName: "arrow.up.circle.fill")
                        .foregroundColor(.blue)
                    Text("\(remaining.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol)) to go (\(Int((current/target)*100))% saved)")
                }
                .font(.subheadline)
                .fontWeight(.medium)
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                .padding(8)
                .background(Color.blue.opacity(0.1))
                .cornerRadius(8)
            }
            
        default:
            EmptyView()
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Computed Properties
    
    private var shouldShowTargetProgress: Bool {
        if liveAccount.accountType == .debt {
            return true
        }
        return liveAccount.targetAmount != nil && liveAccount.targetAmount! > 0
    }
    
    private var shouldShowInstallmentCalculator: Bool {
        guard liveAccount.targetDate != nil && liveAccount.paymentFrequency != nil else {
            return false
        }
        return liveAccount.accountType == .goal || liveAccount.accountType == .debt
    }
    
    private var accountTypeColor: Color {
        switch liveAccount.accountType {
        case .debit:  return .primary
        case .goal:   return .blue
        case .budget: return .purple
        case .debt:   return .orange
        }
    }
    
    private var balanceLabel: String {
        switch liveAccount.accountType {
        case .debit:  return "Current Balance"
        case .goal:   return "Current Savings"
        case .budget: return "Spent This Period"
        case .debt:   return "Amount Owed"
        }
    }
    
    private var balanceColor: Color {
        let balance = data.balance(for: liveAccount)
        
        switch liveAccount.accountType {
        case .debit:
            return balance < 0 ? .red : .primary
            
        case .goal:
            if let target = liveAccount.targetAmount, balance >= target {
                return .green
            }
            return .primary
            
        case .budget:
            if let target = liveAccount.targetAmount {
                return balance > target ? .red : .primary
            }
            return .primary
            
        case .debt:
            return balance > 0 ? .orange : .green
        }
    }
    
    private var balanceSubtitle: String? {
        switch liveAccount.accountType {
        case .debit:
            let change = data.balance(for: liveAccount) - liveAccount.initialAmount
            let prefix = change >= 0 ? "+" : ""
            return "Change from start: \(prefix)\(change.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))"
            
        default:
            return nil
        }
    }
    
    private var progressSectionTitle: String {
        switch liveAccount.accountType {
        case .goal:   return "Goal Progress"
        case .budget: return "Budget Progress"
        case .debt:   return "Payoff Progress"
        default:      return "Progress"
        }
    }
    
    private var currentAmountLabel: String {
        switch liveAccount.accountType {
        case .goal:   return "Saved"
        case .budget: return "Spent"
        case .debt:   return "Owed"
        default:      return "Current"
        }
    }
    
    private var targetAmountLabel: String {
        switch liveAccount.accountType {
        case .goal:   return "Goal"
        case .budget: return "Budget"
        case .debt:   return "Payoff"
        default:      return "Target"
        }
    }
    
    private func progressBarWidth(current: Double, target: Double, totalWidth: CGFloat) -> CGFloat {
        let percentage = min(max(current / target, 0), 1.0)
        return totalWidth * percentage
    }
    
    private func progressBarColor(current: Double, target: Double) -> Color {
        let percentage = current / target
        
        switch liveAccount.accountType {
        case .budget:
            if percentage > 1.0 { return .red }
            else if percentage > 0.9 { return .orange }
            else { return .green }
            
        case .goal:
            return percentage >= 1.0 ? .green : .blue
            
        case .debt:
            return .orange
            
        default:
            return .blue
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Installment Calculation
    
    private func calculateInstallment() -> (periodsRemaining: Int, amountPerPeriod: Double)? {
        guard let targetDate = liveAccount.targetDate,
              let frequency = liveAccount.paymentFrequency else {
            return nil
        }
        
        let today = Date()
        guard targetDate > today else { return nil }
        
        let periodsRemaining = frequency.periodsBetween(from: today, to: targetDate)
        guard periodsRemaining > 0 else { return nil }
        
        let current = data.balance(for: liveAccount)
        let remaining: Double
        
        switch liveAccount.accountType {
        case .goal:
            if let target = liveAccount.targetAmount {
                remaining = max(target - current, 0)
            } else {
                return nil
            }
            
        case .debt:
            remaining = max(current, 0)
            
        default:
            return nil
        }
        
        let amountPerPeriod = remaining / Double(periodsRemaining)
        return (periodsRemaining, amountPerPeriod)
    }
    
    // MARK: - View Helpers
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private var fiscalRangeFooter: some View {
        if fiscalStartDay != 1 {
            let range = getCurrentFiscalRange()
            let rangeText = formatFiscalRange(range)
            Text("Current period: \(rangeText)")
        } else {
            EmptyView()
        }
    }

    private func formatFiscalRange(_ range: (start: Date, end: Date)) -> String {
        let formatter = DateFormatter()
        formatter.dateFormat = "MMM d, yyyy"
        return "\(formatter.string(from: range.start)) - \(formatter.string(from: range.end))"
    }
    
    private func calculateCurrentMonthTotals() -> (inflow: Double, outflow: Double) {
        let range = getCurrentFiscalRange()
        let accountTransactions = data.transactions(for: liveAccount)
        
        let thisMonthTransactions = accountTransactions.filter {
            $0.date >= range.start && $0.date <= range.end
        }
        
        let inflow = thisMonthTransactions
            .filter { $0.type == .inflow }
            .reduce(0) { $0 + $1.amount }
            
        let outflow = thisMonthTransactions
            .filter { $0.type == .outflow }
            .reduce(0) { $0 + $1.amount }
            
        return (inflow, outflow)
    }

    private func getCurrentFiscalRange() -> (start: Date, end: Date) {
        let now = Date()
        let calendar = Calendar.current
        let components = calendar.dateComponents([.year, .month], from: now)
        
        var startComponents = DateComponents()
        startComponents.year = components.year
        startComponents.month = components.month
        startComponents.day = fiscalStartDay
        
        var start = calendar.date(from: startComponents) ?? now
        
        if now < start {
            start = calendar.date(byAdding: .month, value: -1, to: start) ?? start
        }
        
        let end = calendar.date(byAdding: .month, value: 1, to: start)?.addingTimeInterval(-1) ?? now
        return (start, end)
    }
}

// MARK: - Helper View

/// A consistent row format for displaying labeled monetary values.
struct StatRow: View {
    let title: String
    let value: Double
    let symbol: String
    var color: Color = .primary
    
    var body: some View {
        HStack {
            Text(title)
            Spacer()
            Text(value.formattedCurrency(symbol: symbol))
                .foregroundColor(color)
        }
    }
}


==================================================
FILE: Pocket Ledger/Views/Accounts/AddAccountGroupView.swift
==================================================

import SwiftUI

struct AddAccountGroupView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @Environment(\.dismiss) var dismiss
    
    // Optional: If editing, pass the existing group
    let groupToEdit: AccountGroup?
    
    @State private var name: String
    @State private var selectedMemberIDs: Set<UUID>
    @State private var searchText: String = ""
    
    @State private var showingCycleAlert = false
    @State private var cycleAlertMessage = ""
    
    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    
    // MARK: - Focus State
    enum Field {
        case name
        case search
    }
    @FocusState private var focusedField: Field?
    
    // MARK: - Member Display Mode
    enum DisplayMode: String, CaseIterable {
        case all = "All"
        case accounts = "Accounts Only"
        case groups = "Groups Only"
    }
    @State private var displayMode: DisplayMode = .all
    
    // MARK: - Explicit Initializer
    init(groupToEdit: AccountGroup? = nil) {
        self.groupToEdit = groupToEdit
        
        if let group = groupToEdit {
            _name = State(initialValue: group.name)
            _selectedMemberIDs = State(initialValue: Set(group.memberIDs))
        } else {
            _name = State(initialValue: "")
            _selectedMemberIDs = State(initialValue: [])
        }
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                // MARK: - Name Section
                Section(header: Text("Group Name")) {
                    TextField("e.g., Total Cash, Net Worth", text: $name)
                        .focused($focusedField, equals: .name)
                        .submitLabel(.next)
                        .onSubmit {
                            focusedField = .search
                        }
                }
                
                // MARK: - Members Section
                Section(header: memberSectionHeader) {
                    if availableAccounts.isEmpty && availableGroups.isEmpty {
                        Text("No accounts or groups available.")
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    } else {
                        memberList
                    }
                }
            }
            .formToolbar(
                title: groupToEdit == nil ? "New Group" : "Edit Group",
                canSave: canSave,
                onSave: { saveGroup() },
                onCancel: { dismiss() }
            )
            .alert("Cannot Add Group", isPresented: $showingCycleAlert) {
                Button("OK", role: .cancel) { }
            } message: {
                Text(cycleAlertMessage)
            }
            .onAppear {
                if groupToEdit == nil {
                    focusedField = .name
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - View Components
    
    private var memberSectionHeader: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            Text("Members")
            
            // Search Bar
            HStack {
                Image(systemName: "magnifyingglass")
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                TextField("Search accounts...", text: $searchText)
                    .focused($focusedField, equals: .search)
                    .submitLabel(.done)
                    .onSubmit { handleSearchSubmit() }
                
                if !searchText.isEmpty {
                    Button { searchText = "" } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "xmark.circle.fill")
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                }
            }
            .padding(8)
            .background(Color(.secondarySystemBackground))
            .cornerRadius(8)
            
            // Filter Segments
            Picker("Display", selection: $displayMode) {
                ForEach(DisplayMode.allCases, id: \.self) { mode in
                    Text(mode.rawValue).tag(mode)
                }
            }
            .pickerStyle(.segmented)
        }
        .padding(.bottom, 4)
    }
    
    private var memberList: some View {
        Group {
            // 1. Groups List
            if displayMode != .accounts {
                ForEach(filteredGroups) { group in
                    Button {
                        attemptToggleGroup(group)
                    } label: {
                        HStack {
                            ZStack {
                                Circle()
                                    .fill(Color.purple.opacity(0.1))
                                    .frame(width: 32, height: 32)
                                Image(systemName: "folder.fill")
                                    .foregroundColor(.purple)
                                    .font(.caption)
                            }
                            
                            VStack(alignment: .leading) {
                                Text(group.name)
                                    .foregroundColor(.primary)
                                Text("\(group.memberIDs.count) members")
                                    .font(.caption)
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            }
                            
                            Spacer()
                            
                            if selectedMemberIDs.contains(group.id) {
                                Image(systemName: "checkmark.circle.fill")
                                    .foregroundColor(.blue)
                                    .font(.title3)
                            } else {
                                Image(systemName: "circle")
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary.opacity(0.3))
                                    .font(.title3)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            
            // 2. Accounts List
            if displayMode != .groups {
                ForEach(filteredAccounts) { account in
                    let isIndirect = indirectlySelectedMemberIDs.contains(account.id)
                    let isDirect = selectedMemberIDs.contains(account.id)
                    
                    Button {
                        if !isIndirect {
                            toggleSelection(for: account.id)
                        }
                    } label: {
                        HStack {
                            AccountTypeIcon(type: account.accountType, size: 32)
                            
                            VStack(alignment: .leading) {
                                Text(account.name)
                                    .foregroundColor(isIndirect ? .secondary : .primary)
                                
                                if isIndirect {
                                    Text("Included via group")
                                        .font(.caption2)
                                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                        .italic()
                                } else {
                                    Text(data.balance(for: account).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                                        .font(.caption)
                                        .foregroundColor(balanceColor(for: account))
                                }
                            }
                            
                            Spacer()
                            
                            if isDirect {
                                Image(systemName: "checkmark.circle.fill")
                                    .foregroundColor(.blue)
                                    .font(.title3)
                            } else if isIndirect {
                                Image(systemName: "checkmark.circle.fill")
                                    .foregroundColor(.gray.opacity(0.5)) // Gray checkmark for implicit inclusion
                                    .font(.title3)
                            } else {
                                Image(systemName: "circle")
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary.opacity(0.3))
                                    .font(.title3)
                            }
                        }
                        .contentShape(Rectangle())
                    }
                    .disabled(isIndirect) // Prevent selecting if already included
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Logic & Helpers
    
    private var canSave: Bool {
        !name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty && !selectedMemberIDs.isEmpty
    }
    
    private var availableAccounts: [Account] {
        data.accounts.filter { !$0.isArchived }
            .sorted { $0.name < $1.name }
    }
    
    private var availableGroups: [AccountGroup] {
        data.accountGroups
            .filter { $0.id != groupToEdit?.id } // Cannot add self
            .sorted { $0.name < $1.name }
    }
    
    private var filteredAccounts: [Account] {
        if searchText.isEmpty { return availableAccounts }
        return availableAccounts.filter { $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(searchText) }
    }
    
    private var filteredGroups: [AccountGroup] {
        if searchText.isEmpty { return availableGroups }
        return availableGroups.filter { $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(searchText) }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Smart Selection Logic
    
    /// Returns IDs of accounts that are already included inside the GROUPS currently selected.
    /// Used to disable/gray out individual accounts to prevent redundancy.
    private var indirectlySelectedMemberIDs: Set<UUID> {
        var indirectIDs = Set<UUID>()
        
        // 1. Identify which selected IDs are actually Groups
        let selectedGroupIDs = selectedMemberIDs.filter { id in
            data.accountGroups.contains(where: { $0.id == id })
        }
        
        // 2. Harvest all children from those groups
        for groupID in selectedGroupIDs {
            if let group = data.accountGroups.first(where: { $0.id == groupID }) {
                // Use our new recursive helper from AppData/AccountGroup
                let children = group.allAccountIDs(in: data.accountGroups, accounts: data.accounts)
                indirectIDs.formUnion(children)
            }
        }
        
        return indirectIDs
    }
    
    private func toggleSelection(for id: UUID) {
        if selectedMemberIDs.contains(id) {
            selectedMemberIDs.remove(id)
        } else {
            selectedMemberIDs.insert(id)
        }
    }
    
    private func attemptToggleGroup(_ candidate: AccountGroup) {
        // 1. Deselection is always safe
        if selectedMemberIDs.contains(candidate.id) {
            selectedMemberIDs.remove(candidate.id)
            return
        }
        
        // 2. Cycle Detection
        // Create a temporary version of the group we are building to check for cycles
        let currentGroupID = groupToEdit?.id ?? UUID()
        let tempGroup = AccountGroup(id: currentGroupID, name: name, memberIDs: Array(selectedMemberIDs))
        
        if tempGroup.wouldCreateCycle(byAdding: candidate.id, in: data.accountGroups) {
            cycleAlertMessage = "Adding '\(candidate.name)' would create a circular reference (it already contains this group)."
            showingCycleAlert = true
            return
        }
        
        // 3. Redundancy Cleanup
        // If we add this group, we should remove any individual accounts currently selected
        // that are INSIDE this candidate group, to prevent double-listing.
        let childrenOfCandidate = candidate.allAccountIDs(in: data.accountGroups, accounts: data.accounts)
        
        // Remove direct account selections if they are covered by this new group
        let redundancy = selectedMemberIDs.intersection(childrenOfCandidate)
        if !redundancy.isEmpty {
            selectedMemberIDs.subtract(redundancy)
        }
        
        // 4. Add the group
        selectedMemberIDs.insert(candidate.id)
    }
    
    private func handleSearchSubmit() {
        if searchText.isEmpty {
            if canSave {
                saveGroup()
            }
        } else if filteredAccounts.count + filteredGroups.count == 1 {
            // Auto-select single result
            if let account = filteredAccounts.first {
                if !indirectlySelectedMemberIDs.contains(account.id) {
                    toggleSelection(for: account.id)
                }
            } else if let group = filteredGroups.first {
                attemptToggleGroup(group)
            }
            searchText = ""
        }
    }
    
    private func saveGroup() {
        if var group = groupToEdit {
            group.name = name
            group.memberIDs = Array(selectedMemberIDs)
            data.updateAccountGroup(group)
        } else {
            data.addAccountGroup(name: name, memberIDs: Array(selectedMemberIDs))
        }
        dismiss()
    }
    
    // MARK: - Styling Helpers
    
    private func balanceColor(for account: Account) -> Color {
        let balance = data.balance(for: account)
        
        switch account.accountType {
        case .debit:
            return balance < 0 ? .red : .primary
        case .goal:
            if let target = account.targetAmount, balance >= target {
                return .green
            }
            return .primary
        case .budget:
            if let target = account.targetAmount, balance > target {
                return .red
            }
            return .primary
        case .debt:
            return balance > 0 ? .orange : .green
        }
    }
}

//
//  AccountStatusBadge.swift
//  Pocket Ledger
//
//  Created by Zhi Zheng Yeo on 25/1/26.

import SwiftUI

// MARK: - Account Status Badge

enum AccountStatusBadge {
    case goalMet
    case budgetExceeded
    case archived
    
    var icon: String {
        switch self {
        case .goalMet:        return "checkmark.circle.fill"
        case .budgetExceeded: return "exclamationmark.triangle.fill"
        case .archived:       return "archivebox.fill"
        }
    }
    
    var color: Color {
        switch self {
        case .goalMet:        return .green
        case .budgetExceeded: return .red
        case .archived:       return .secondary
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Account Status Helper

extension Account {
    /// Determines if account should show a status badge
    func statusBadge(balance: Double) -> AccountStatusBadge? {
        if isArchived {
            return .archived
        }
        
        switch accountType {
        case .goal:
            if let target = targetAmount, balance >= target {
                return .goalMet
            }
        case .budget:
            if let target = targetAmount, balance > target {
                return .budgetExceeded
            }
        default:
            break
        }
        
        return nil
    }
}

// MARK: - Compact Account Badge View

struct AccountStatusBadgeView: View {
    let badge: AccountStatusBadge
    
    var body: some View {
        Image(systemName: badge.icon)
            .font(.caption2)
            .foregroundColor(badge.color)
    }
}

// MARK: - Account Type Icon View

struct AccountTypeIcon: View {
    let type: AccountType
    let size: CGFloat
    
    init(type: AccountType, size: CGFloat = 20) {
        self.type = type
        self.size = size
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        Image(systemName: type.icon)
            .font(.system(size: size * 0.6))
            .foregroundColor(type.color)
            .frame(width: size, height: size)
            .background(type.color.opacity(0.15))
            .clipShape(Circle())
    }
}

FILE: Pocket Ledger/Views/Accounts/AccountGroupDetailView.swift

import SwiftUI

struct AccountGroupDetailView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    let group: AccountGroup
    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    @AppStorage("fiscalStartDay") private var fiscalStartDay: Int = 1
    
    @State private var expandedMemberIDs: Set<UUID> = []
    
    var body: some View {
        List {
            // SECTION 1: Total Balance
            Section(header: Text("Total")) {
                VStack(spacing: 8) {
                    HStack {
                        Text("Group Balance")
                            .font(.headline)
                        Spacer()
                    }
                    
                    Text(data.groupBalance(group).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                        .font(.system(size: 36, weight: .bold, design: .rounded))
                        .foregroundColor(totalBalanceColor)
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                    
                    // Nesting depth indicator
                    let depth = data.depth(of: group)
                    if depth > 0 {
                        HStack(spacing: 4) {
                            Image(systemName: "arrow.down.forward")
                                .font(.caption2)
                            Text("Nesting depth: \(depth)")
                                .font(.caption)
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                    }
                }
            }
            
            // SECTION 2: Hierarchical Breakdown
            Section(header: HStack {
                Text("Members (\(group.memberIDs.count))")
                Spacer()
                Button(expandedMemberIDs.isEmpty ? "Expand All" : "Collapse All") {
                    toggleAllMembers()
                }
                .font(.caption)
            }, footer: Text(balanceCalculationExplanation)) {
                // REFACTORED: Now uses a struct instead of a recursive function call
                ForEach(data.members(for: group), id: \.id) { member in
                    MemberRowView(
                        member: member,
                        depth: 0,
                        expandedMemberIDs: $expandedMemberIDs
                    )
                }
            }
            
            // SECTION 3: Statistics (This Period)
            Section(header: Text("Statistics")) {
                let totals = calculateGroupMonthlyTotals()
                StatRow(title: "Inflow (This Month)", value: totals.inflow, symbol: currencySymbol, color: .green)
                StatRow(title: "Outflow (This Month)", value: totals.outflow, symbol: currencySymbol, color: .red)
            }
            
            // SECTION 4: Recent Transactions
            Section(header: Text("Recent Transactions (Last 25)")) {
                let recent = recentTransactions
                
                if recent.isEmpty {
                    Text("No transactions in this group.")
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                } else {
                    ForEach(recent) { tx in
                        NavigationLink(destination: TransactionDetailView(transaction: tx)) {
                            TransactionRow(transaction: tx)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        .navigationTitle(group.name)
        .onAppear {
            // Auto-expand first level
            expandedMemberIDs = Set(group.memberIDs.filter { id in
                data.accountGroups.contains(where: { $0.id == id })
            })
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Helper Functions
    
    private func toggleAllMembers() {
        if expandedMemberIDs.isEmpty {
            // Expand all groups recursively
            expandedMemberIDs = Set(allGroupIDs(in: group))
        } else {
            // Collapse all
            expandedMemberIDs.removeAll()
        }
    }
    
    private func allGroupIDs(in group: AccountGroup) -> [UUID] {
        var result: [UUID] = []
        
        for member in data.members(for: group) {
            if case .group(let childGroup) = member {
                result.append(childGroup.id)
                result.append(contentsOf: allGroupIDs(in: childGroup))
            }
        }
        
        return result
    }
    
    private var totalBalanceColor: Color {
        let total = data.groupBalance(group)
        return total < 0 ? .red : .primary
    }
    
    private var balanceCalculationExplanation: String {
        let allAccountIDs = data.allAccountIDs(in: group)
        let hasDebit = allAccountIDs.contains { id in
            data.accounts.first(where: { $0.id == id })?.isCredit == false
        }
        let hasCredit = allAccountIDs.contains { id in
            data.accounts.first(where: { $0.id == id })?.isCredit == true
        }
        
        if hasDebit && hasCredit {
            return "Group total = Debit accounts (+) minus Credit accounts (−). Groups are calculated recursively."
        } else if hasCredit {
            return "Group total = Sum of all credit balances. Groups are calculated recursively."
        } else {
            return "Group total = Sum of all debit balances. Groups are calculated recursively."
        }
    }
    
    private var recentTransactions: [Transaction] {
        let allAccountIDs = data.allAccountIDs(in: group)
        
        return data.transactions
            .filter { allAccountIDs.contains($0.accountID) }
            .sorted { $0.date > $1.date }
            .prefix(25)
            .map { $0 }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Statistics Calculation
    
    private func calculateGroupMonthlyTotals() -> (inflow: Double, outflow: Double) {
        let allAccountIDs = data.allAccountIDs(in: group)
        let transactions = data.transactions.filter { allAccountIDs.contains($0.accountID) }
        
        let range = getCurrentFiscalRange()
        let filtered = transactions.filter { $0.date >= range.start && $0.date <= range.end }
        
        let inflow = filtered.filter { $0.type == .inflow }.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.amount }
        let outflow = filtered.filter { $0.type == .outflow }.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.amount }
        return (inflow, outflow)
    }

    private func getCurrentFiscalRange() -> (start: Date, end: Date) {
        let calendar = Calendar.current
        let components = calendar.dateComponents([.year, .month], from: Date())
        let startComp = DateComponents(year: components.year, month: components.month, day: fiscalStartDay)
        var start = calendar.date(from: startComp) ?? Date()
        if Date() < start { start = calendar.date(byAdding: .month, value: -1, to: start) ?? start }
        let end = calendar.date(byAdding: .month, value: 1, to: start)?.addingTimeInterval(-1) ?? Date()
        return (start, end)
    }
}

// MARK: - Member Row Struct (Fixes Recursive Type Error)

struct MemberRowView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    let member: MemberType
    let depth: Int
    @Binding var expandedMemberIDs: Set<UUID>
    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    
    var body: some View {
        switch member {
        case .account(let account):
            accountRow(account)
        case .group(let group):
            groupRow(group)
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Account Member Row
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private func accountRow(_ account: Account) -> some View {
        NavigationLink(destination: AccountDetailView(account: account)) {
            HStack(spacing: 8) {
                // Indentation
                if depth > 0 {
                    indentationView(depth: depth)
                }
                
                // Account icon
                AccountTypeIcon(type: account.accountType, size: 28)
                
                // Account info
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                    HStack(spacing: 6) {
                        Text(account.name)
                            .font(.body)
                        
                        if let badge = account.statusBadge(balance: data.balance(for: account)) {
                            AccountStatusBadgeView(badge: badge)
                        }
                    }
                    
                    Text(account.accountType.rawValue)
                        .font(.caption)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                }
                
                Spacer()
                
                // Balance breakdown
                VStack(alignment: .trailing, spacing: 4) {
                    Text(data.balance(for: account).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                        .font(.body)
                        .fontWeight(.medium)
                        .foregroundColor(accountBalanceColor(for: account))
                    
                    Text(contributionText(for: account))
                        .font(.caption)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                }
            }
            .padding(.vertical, 4)
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Group Member Row (Recursive)
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private func groupRow(_ childGroup: AccountGroup) -> some View {
        VStack(spacing: 0) {
            // Group header (expandable)
            Button {
                toggleExpansion(for: childGroup.id)
            } label: {
                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                    // Indentation
                    if depth > 0 {
                        indentationView(depth: depth)
                    }
                    
                    // Expansion chevron
                    Image(systemName: expandedMemberIDs.contains(childGroup.id) ? "chevron.down" : "chevron.right")
                        .font(.caption)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        .frame(width: 20)
                    
                    // Group icon
                    Image(systemName: "folder.fill")
                        .font(.title3)
                        .foregroundColor(.purple)
                        .frame(width: 28, height: 28)
                    
                    // Group info
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                        Text(childGroup.name)
                            .font(.body)
                            .fontWeight(.medium)
                            .foregroundColor(.purple)
                        
                        Text("\(childGroup.memberIDs.count) members")
                            .font(.caption)
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                    
                    Spacer()
                    
                    // Group balance
                    Text(data.groupBalance(childGroup).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                        .font(.body)
                        .fontWeight(.medium)
                        .foregroundColor(.purple)
                }
                .contentShape(Rectangle())
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
            .padding(.vertical, 4)
            
            // Expanded children (recursive)
            // This is now safe because MemberRowView is a struct (concrete type)
            if expandedMemberIDs.contains(childGroup.id) {
                ForEach(data.members(for: childGroup), id: \.id) { nestedMember in
                    MemberRowView(
                        member: nestedMember,
                        depth: depth + 1,
                        expandedMemberIDs: $expandedMemberIDs
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Helpers
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private func indentationView(depth: Int) -> some View {
        HStack(spacing: 2) {
            ForEach(0..<depth, id: \.self) { _ in
                Rectangle()
                    .fill(Color.secondary.opacity(0.2))
                    .frame(width: 2, height: 24)
                    .padding(.leading, 8)
            }
        }
    }
    
    private func toggleExpansion(for id: UUID) {
        if expandedMemberIDs.contains(id) {
            expandedMemberIDs.remove(id)
        } else {
            expandedMemberIDs.insert(id)
        }
    }
    
    private func accountBalanceColor(for account: Account) -> Color {
        let balance = data.balance(for: account)
        
        if account.isCredit {
            if balance > account.initialAmount * 0.9 {
                return .red
            } else if balance > account.initialAmount * 0.7 {
                return .orange
            }
        } else {
            if balance < 0 {
                return .red
            }
        }
        return .primary
    }
    
    private func contributionText(for account: Account) -> String {
        let balance = data.balance(for: account)
        
        if account.isCredit {
            return "(\(balance.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol)))"
        } else {
            return "+\(balance.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))"
        }
    }
}
