import SwiftUI

struct AccountsListView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    
    // MARK: - Navigation State (Bindings from ContentView)
    // These replace the local @State so shortcuts can trigger them
    @Binding var globalAddAccountTrigger: Bool
    @Binding var globalAddGroupTrigger: Bool
    
    // Local State for Editing (Sheets that don't need global shortcuts)
    @State private var accountToEdit: Account?
    @State private var groupToEdit: AccountGroup? = nil
    
    // MARK: - Expansion States
    @State private var isDebitExpanded = true
    @State private var isGoalsExpanded = true
    @State private var isBudgetsExpanded = true
    @State private var isDebtsExpanded = true
    @State private var isGroupsExpanded = true
    @State private var isArchivedExpanded = false

    // MARK: - Filtered Lists
    
    private var debitAccounts: [Account] {
        data.accounts
            .filter { $0.accountType == .debit && !$0.isArchived }
            .sorted { $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveCompare($1.name) == .orderedAscending }
    }
    
    private var goalAccounts: [Account] {
        data.accounts
            .filter { $0.accountType == .goal && !$0.isArchived }
            .sorted { $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveCompare($1.name) == .orderedAscending }
    }
    
    private var budgetAccounts: [Account] {
        data.accounts
            .filter { $0.accountType == .budget && !$0.isArchived }
            .sorted { $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveCompare($1.name) == .orderedAscending }
    }

    private var debtAccounts: [Account] {
        data.accounts
            .filter { $0.accountType == .debt && !$0.isArchived }
            .sorted { $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveCompare($1.name) == .orderedAscending }
    }
    
    private var archivedAccounts: [Account] {
        data.accounts
            .filter { $0.isArchived }
            .sorted { ($0.archivedDate ?? Date.distantPast) > ($1.archivedDate ?? Date.distantPast) }
    }

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            ZStack(alignment: .bottomTrailing) {
                List {
                    if data.accounts.isEmpty && data.accountGroups.isEmpty {
                        Text("No accounts yet. Tap + to add one.")
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            .padding()
                    }

                    // SECTION 1: DEBIT
                    if !debitAccounts.isEmpty {
                        DisclosureGroup(
                            isExpanded: $isDebitExpanded,
                            content: {
                                ForEach(debitAccounts) { account in
                                    accountRowLink(for: account)
                                }
                            },
                            label: { headerLabel(title: "Debit", count: debitAccounts.count) }
                        )
                    }

                    // SECTION 2: GOALS
                    if !goalAccounts.isEmpty {
                        DisclosureGroup(
                            isExpanded: $isGoalsExpanded,
                            content: {
                                ForEach(goalAccounts) { account in
                                    accountRowLink(for: account)
                                }
                            },
                            label: { headerLabel(title: "Goals", count: goalAccounts.count) }
                        )
                    }
                    
                    // SECTION 3: BUDGETS
                    if !budgetAccounts.isEmpty {
                        DisclosureGroup(
                            isExpanded: $isBudgetsExpanded,
                            content: {
                                ForEach(budgetAccounts) { account in
                                    accountRowLink(for: account)
                                }
                            },
                            label: { headerLabel(title: "Budgets", count: budgetAccounts.count) }
                        )
                    }
                    
                    // SECTION 4: DEBTS
                    if !debtAccounts.isEmpty {
                        DisclosureGroup(
                            isExpanded: $isDebtsExpanded,
                            content: {
                                ForEach(debtAccounts) { account in
                                    accountRowLink(for: account)
                                }
                            },
                            label: { headerLabel(title: "Debts", count: debtAccounts.count) }
                        )
                    }
                    
                    // SECTION 5: ACCOUNT GROUPS
                    if !data.accountGroups.isEmpty {
                        DisclosureGroup(
                            isExpanded: $isGroupsExpanded,
                            content: {
                                ForEach(data.accountGroups) { group in
                                    NavigationLink(destination: AccountGroupDetailView(group: group)) {
                                        HStack {
                                            Image(systemName: "folder")
                                                .foregroundColor(.blue)
                                            Text(group.name)
                                                .fontWeight(.medium)
                                            Spacer()
                                            Text(data.groupBalance(group).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                        }
                                    }
                                    .swipeActions(edge: .leading) {
                                        Button {
                                            groupToEdit = group
                                        } label: {
                                            Label("Edit", systemImage: "pencil")
                                        }
                                        .tint(.blue)
                                    }
                                    .swipeActions(edge: .trailing) {
                                        Button(role: .destructive) {
                                            data.deleteAccountGroup(group)
                                        } label: {
                                            Label("Delete", systemImage: "trash")
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            },
                            label: { headerLabel(title: "Groups", count: data.accountGroups.count) }
                        )
                    }
                    
                    // SECTION 6: ARCHIVED
                    if !archivedAccounts.isEmpty {
                        DisclosureGroup(
                            isExpanded: $isArchivedExpanded,
                            content: {
                                ForEach(archivedAccounts) { account in
                                    NavigationLink(destination: AccountDetailView(account: account)) {
                                        HStack {
                                            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                                                Text(account.name)
                                                    .font(.headline)
                                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                                
                                                HStack(spacing: 4) {
                                                    Image(systemName: "archivebox.fill")
                                                        .font(.caption2)
                                                    Text("Archived")
                                                        .font(.caption)
                                                }
                                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                            }
                                            Spacer()
                                            Text(data.balance(for: account).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                                .opacity(0.6)
                                        }
                                    }
                                    .swipeActions(edge: .trailing) {
                                        Button(role: .destructive) { data.deleteAccount(account) } label: { Label("Delete", systemImage: "trash") }
                                    }
                                }
                            },
                            label: { headerLabel(title: "Archived", count: archivedAccounts.count) }
                        )
                    }

                    // Footer spacer
                    Color.clear.frame(height: 80).listRowBackground(Color.clear)
                }
                
                // FAB Menu (Modified to use Bindings)
                Menu {
                    Button {
                        globalAddAccountTrigger = true
                    } label: {
                        Label("New Account", systemImage: "plus")
                    }
                    
                    Button {
                        globalAddGroupTrigger = true
                    } label: {
                        Label("New Account Group", systemImage: "folder.badge.plus")
                    }
                } label: {
                    Image(systemName: "plus")
                        .font(.title2.weight(.bold))
                        .foregroundColor(.white)
                        .frame(width: 56, height: 56)
                        .background(Color.blue)
                        .clipShape(Circle())
                        .shadow(color: .black.opacity(0.3), radius: 4, x: 0, y: 4)
                }
                .padding()
            }
            .navigationTitle("Accounts")
            .toolbar {
                // COLLAPSE/EXPAND ALL BUTTON
                ToolbarItem(placement: .navigationBarLeading) {
                    Button {
                        toggleAllSections()
                    } label: {
                        let anyExpanded = isDebitExpanded || isGoalsExpanded || isBudgetsExpanded || isDebtsExpanded || isGroupsExpanded
                        Image(systemName: anyExpanded ? "arrow.up.left.and.arrow.down.right" : "arrow.down.right.and.arrow.up.left")
                            .font(.subheadline)
                    }
                }
            }
            // Sheets (Bound to Global State)
            .sheet(isPresented: $globalAddAccountTrigger) {
                AddAccountView()
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: $globalAddGroupTrigger) {
                AddAccountGroupView()
            }
            // Sheets (Bound to Local State)
            .sheet(item: $accountToEdit) { account in
                EditAccountView(account: account)
            }
            .sheet(item: $groupToEdit) { group in
                AddAccountGroupView(groupToEdit: group)
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Logic
    
    private func toggleAllSections() {
        withAnimation {
            let anyExpanded = isDebitExpanded || isGoalsExpanded || isBudgetsExpanded || isDebtsExpanded || isGroupsExpanded
            let newState = !anyExpanded
            isDebitExpanded = newState
            isGoalsExpanded = newState
            isBudgetsExpanded = newState
            isDebtsExpanded = newState
            isGroupsExpanded = newState
        }
    }

    // MARK: - View Builders
    
    private func headerLabel(title: String, count: Int) -> some View {
        HStack {
            Text(title)
                .font(.headline)
                .foregroundColor(.primary)
            Spacer()
            Text("\(count)")
                .font(.caption)
                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                .background(Color(.secondarySystemBackground))
                .clipShape(Capsule())
        }
    }
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private func accountRowLink(for account: Account) -> some View {
        NavigationLink(destination: AccountDetailView(account: account)) {
            AccountRow(account: account)
        }
        .swipeActions(edge: .leading) {
            Button {
                accountToEdit = account
            } label: {
                Label("Edit", systemImage: "pencil")
            }
            .tint(.blue)
        }
        .swipeActions(edge: .trailing) {
            Button(role: .destructive) {
                data.deleteAccount(account)
            } label: {
                Label("Delete", systemImage: "trash")
            }
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Account Row

struct AccountRow: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    let account: Account

    var body: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 8) {
            // 1. LEFT SIDE: Name & Type
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 3) {
                // Row A: Name + Badge
                HStack(spacing: 6) {
                    Text(account.name)
                        .font(.body)
                        .fontWeight(.medium)
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                    
                    if let badge = account.statusBadge(balance: currentBalance) {
                        AccountStatusBadgeView(badge: badge)
                            .scaleEffect(0.75)
                    }
                }
                
                // Row B: Icon + Label (Color Restored)
                HStack(spacing: 4) {
                    // FIX: Apply the specific type color to the icon
                    Image(systemName: account.accountType.icon)
                        .foregroundColor(account.accountType.color)
                    
                    Text(accountTypeLabel)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                }
                .font(.caption)
            }
            
            Spacer()
            
            // 2. RIGHT SIDE: Balance
            balanceView
                .font(.system(.body, design: .monospaced))
                .multilineTextAlignment(.trailing)
        }
        .padding(.vertical, 6)
    }
    
    // MARK: - Balance View
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private var balanceView: some View {
        switch account.accountType {
        case .debit:
            Text(currentBalance.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                .foregroundColor(balanceColor)
                
        case .goal:
            if let target = account.targetAmount {
                let isMet = currentBalance >= target
                VStack(alignment: .trailing, spacing: 0) {
                    Text(currentBalance.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                        .foregroundColor(isMet ? .green : .primary)
                    
                    Text("of \(target.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))")
                        .font(.caption2)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                }
            } else {
                 Text(currentBalance.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
            }
            
        case .budget:
            if let target = account.targetAmount {
                let isOverBudget = currentBalance > target
                VStack(alignment: .trailing, spacing: 0) {
                    Text(currentBalance.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                        .foregroundColor(isOverBudget ? .red : .primary)
                    
                    Text("/ \(target.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))")
                        .font(.caption2)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                }
            } else {
                Text(currentBalance.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
            }
            
        case .debt:
            Text(currentBalance.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                .foregroundColor(currentBalance > 0 ? .orange : .green)
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Computed Properties
    
    private var currentBalance: Double {
        data.balance(for: account)
    }
    
    private var accountTypeLabel: String {
        account.accountType.rawValue.capitalized
    }
    
    private var balanceColor: Color {
        switch account.accountType {
        case .debit:
            return currentBalance < 0 ? .red : .primary
        default:
            return .primary
        }
    }
}


==================================================
FILE: Pocket Ledger/Views/Accounts/EditAccountView.swift
==================================================

import SwiftUI

struct EditAccountView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @Environment(\.dismiss) var dismiss
    var account: Account
    
    @State private var name: String
    @State private var initialAmountString: String
    @State private var targetAmountString: String
    @State private var hasTargetDate: Bool
    @State private var targetDate: Date
    @State private var paymentFrequency: PaymentFrequency
    
    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    
    // MARK: - Focus State
    enum Field: Hashable {
        case name
        case amount
        case target
    }
    
    @FocusState private var focusedField: Field?

    init(account: Account) {
        self.account = account
        _name = State(initialValue: account.name)
        _initialAmountString = State(initialValue: String(format: "%.2f", account.initialAmount))
        
        // Initialize target
        if let target = account.targetAmount {
            // Show 0.00 if it's a debt account with explicit 0 target, or any positive target
            _targetAmountString = State(initialValue: String(format: "%.2f", target))
        } else {
            // Nil target means empty string
            _targetAmountString = State(initialValue: "")
        }
        
        // Initialize target date and frequency
        _hasTargetDate = State(initialValue: account.targetDate != nil)
        _targetDate = State(initialValue: account.targetDate ?? Calendar.current.date(byAdding: .year, value: 1, to: Date()) ?? Date())
        _paymentFrequency = State(initialValue: account.paymentFrequency ?? .monthly)
    }

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                // SECTION 1: Account Type (Read-Only)
                Section(footer: Text("Account type cannot be changed after creation. Create a new account if you need a different type.")) {
                    HStack {
                        Image(systemName: account.accountType.icon)
                            .foregroundColor(accountTypeColor)
                        Text(account.accountType.rawValue + " Account")
                            .foregroundColor(accountTypeColor)
                        Spacer()
                    }
                }
                
                // SECTION 2: Account Details
                Section(header: Text("Account Details")) {
                    TextField("Name", text: $name)
                        .submitLabel(.next)
                        .focused($focusedField, equals: .name)
                        .onSubmit {
                            focusedField = .amount
                        }
                    
                    HStack {
                        Text("Starting Balance")
                        Spacer()
                        TextField("0.00", text: $initialAmountString)
                            .keyboardType(.numbersAndPunctuation) // Changed for math support
                            .multilineTextAlignment(.trailing)
                            .focused($focusedField, equals: .amount)
                            .onSubmit {
                                focusedField = .target
                            }
                    }
                }
                
                // SECTION 3: Target Amount
                Section(header: Text(targetSectionHeader), footer: Text(targetSectionFooter)) {
                    HStack {
                        Text("Target")
                        Spacer()
                        TextField(targetPlaceholder, text: $targetAmountString)
                            .keyboardType(.numbersAndPunctuation) // Changed for math support
                            .multilineTextAlignment(.trailing)
                            .focused($focusedField, equals: .target)
                            .onSubmit {
                                saveChanges()
                            }
                    }
                }
                
                // SECTION 4: Payment/Contribution Plan
                if shouldShowInstallmentSection {
                    Section(header: Text(installmentSectionHeader), footer: Text(installmentSectionFooter)) {
                        Toggle("Set \(account.isCredit ? "Payment" : "Contribution") Plan", isOn: $hasTargetDate)
                        
                        if hasTargetDate {
                            DatePicker("Target Date",
                                     selection: $targetDate,
                                     in: Date()...,
                                     displayedComponents: .date)
                            
                            Picker("Frequency", selection: $paymentFrequency) {
                                ForEach(PaymentFrequency.allCases) { frequency in
                                    Text(frequency.rawValue).tag(frequency)
                                }
                            }
                            
                            // Preview calculation
                            if let preview = calculatePreview() {
                                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                                    Divider()
                                    
                                    HStack {
                                        Image(systemName: "calendar")
                                            .foregroundColor(.blue)
                                        Text("Updated Plan")
                                            .font(.subheadline)
                                            .fontWeight(.semibold)
                                    }
                                    
                                    HStack {
                                        Text("Payments Remaining:")
                                        Spacer()
                                        Text("\(preview.periods)")
                                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                    }
                                    .font(.caption)
                                    
                                    HStack {
                                        Text("Amount per \(paymentFrequency.rawValue.lowercased()):")
                                        Spacer()
                                        Text(preview.amountPerPeriod.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                                            .foregroundColor(account.isCredit ? .orange : .blue)
                                            .fontWeight(.semibold)
                                    }
                                    .font(.caption)
                                }
                                .padding(.vertical, 8)
                                .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                                .background(Color(.systemGray6))
                                .cornerRadius(8)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                // SECTION 5: Current Status
                Section(header: Text("Current Status"), footer: Text("Current balance is calculated from your starting balance plus all transactions.")) {
                    HStack {
                        Text("Current Balance")
                        Spacer()
                        Text(data.balance(for: account).formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                            .foregroundColor(currentBalanceColor)
                    }
                    
                    // Progress indicator
                    if let target = Double(targetAmountString), target > 0 {
                        HStack {
                            Text("Progress")
                            Spacer()
                            Text(progressText)
                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItemGroup(placement: .keyboard) {
                    Spacer()
                    Button("Done") {
                        UIApplication.shared.sendAction(#selector(UIResponder.resignFirstResponder), to: nil, from: nil, for: nil)
                    }
                }
            }
            .formToolbar(
                title: "Edit Account",
                canSave: canSave,
                onSave: {
                    saveChanges()
                },
                onCancel: {
                    dismiss()
                }
            )
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Computed Properties
    
    private var accountTypeColor: Color {
        switch account.accountType {
        case .debit:  return .primary
        case .goal:   return .blue
        case .budget: return .purple
        case .debt:   return .orange
        }
    }
    
    private var targetSectionHeader: String {
        switch account.accountType {
        case .debit:  return "Savings Goal (Optional)"
        case .goal:   return "Savings Goal"
        case .budget: return "Monthly Budget Limit"
        case .debt:   return "Payoff Goal"
        }
    }
    
    private var targetSectionFooter: String {
        switch account.accountType {
        case .debit:
            return "Add a target to convert this into a Goal account for tracking savings progress."
        case .goal:
            return "Your savings target. Progress is tracked automatically."
        case .budget:
            return "Your monthly spending limit. You'll see warnings when approaching this amount."
        case .debt:
            return "Set to $0 to track debt payoff progress. Set higher to create a monthly spending budget."
        }
    }
    
    private var targetPlaceholder: String {
        switch account.accountType {
        case .debit, .goal: return "Target Amount"
        case .budget:       return "Monthly Limit"
        case .debt:         return "0"
        }
    }
    
    private var shouldShowInstallmentSection: Bool {
        switch account.accountType {
        case .goal, .debt:
            return true
        case .budget:
            return false // Budgets don't use installment plans (they're monthly)
        case .debit:
            let target = MathParser.calculate(targetAmountString)
            return target > 0 // Only show if user adds a target
        }
    }
    
    private var installmentSectionHeader: String {
        account.isCredit ? "Payment Plan (Optional)" : "Contribution Plan (Optional)"
    }
    
    private var installmentSectionFooter: String {
        switch account.accountType {
        case .goal:
            return "Set a target date to calculate how much you need to save each period to reach your goal."
        case .debt:
            return "Set a target date to calculate required payments to pay off this debt."
        default:
            return ""
        }
    }
    
    private var currentBalanceColor: Color {
        let balance = data.balance(for: account)
        
        switch account.accountType {
        case .debit:
            return balance < 0 ? .red : .primary
        case .goal:
            if let target = Double(targetAmountString), balance >= target {
                return .green
            }
            return .primary
        case .budget:
            if let target = Double(targetAmountString), balance > target {
                return .red
            }
            return .primary
        case .debt:
            return balance > 0 ? .orange : .green
        }
    }
    
    private var progressText: String {
        let target = MathParser.calculate(targetAmountString)
        guard target > 0 else { return "" }
        
        let current = data.balance(for: account)
        let percentage = (current / target) * 100
        
        switch account.accountType {
        case .goal:
            if current >= target {
                return "Goal reached! 🎉"
            } else {
                return String(format: "%.0f%% of goal reached", percentage)
            }
        case .budget:
            return String(format: "%.0f%% of budget used", percentage)
        case .debt:
            return String(format: "%.0f%% of debt remaining", percentage)
        default:
            return ""
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Validation
    
    private var canSave: Bool {
        !name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty
    }
    
    // MARK: - Calculations
    
    private func calculatePreview() -> (periods: Int, amountPerPeriod: Double)? {
        guard hasTargetDate else { return nil }
        
        let current = data.balance(for: account)
        let target = MathParser.calculate(targetAmountString)
        
        let today = Date()
        guard targetDate > today else { return nil }
        
        let periods = paymentFrequency.periodsBetween(from: today, to: targetDate)
        guard periods > 0 else { return nil }
        
        let remaining: Double
        
        switch account.accountType {
        case .goal:
            // Calculate savings needed
            remaining = max(target - current, 0)
        case .debt:
            // Calculate payoff amount
            remaining = max(current, 0)
        default:
            return nil
        }
        
        let amountPerPeriod = remaining / Double(periods)
        return (periods, amountPerPeriod)
    }
    
    // MARK: - Actions
    
    private func saveChanges() {
        let amount = MathParser.calculate(initialAmountString)
        let targetRaw = MathParser.calculate(targetAmountString)
        
        // Parse target: empty string or "0" → nil for debt accounts
        let target: Double?
        if isTargetEmptyOrZero(targetAmountString, val: targetRaw) {
            target = nil
        } else {
            target = targetRaw
        }
        
        data.updateAccount(
            id: account.id,
            name: name,
            initialAmount: amount,
            targetAmount: target,
            targetDate: hasTargetDate ? targetDate : nil,
            paymentFrequency: hasTargetDate ? paymentFrequency : nil
        )
        dismiss()
    }
    
    /// Helper to determine if we should treat the target as nil
    private func isTargetEmptyOrZero(_ str: String, val: Double) -> Bool {
        if str.isEmpty { return true }
        if !account.isCredit && val == 0 { return true } // Debit/Goal: 0 target = nil
        // For Credit/Debt: explicit 0 is a valid target (payoff goal), so we keep it unless empty
        return false
    }
}

FILE: Pocket Ledger/Views/Accounts/AddAccountView.swift

import SwiftUI

/// Form to add a new account with a name, starting balance, and optional target/installment plan.
struct AddAccountView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @Environment(\.dismiss) var dismiss
    
    @State private var name: String = ""
    @State private var initialAmountString: String = ""
    @State private var isCredit: Bool = false
    @State private var targetAmountString: String = ""
    @State private var hasTargetDate: Bool = false
    @State private var targetDate: Date = Calendar.current.date(byAdding: .year, value: 1, to: Date()) ?? Date()
    @State private var paymentFrequency: PaymentFrequency = .monthly
    
    @State private var selectedGroupIDs: Set<UUID> = []
    
    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    
    // MARK: - Focus State
    enum Field: Hashable {
        case name
        case amount
        case target
    }
    
    @FocusState private var focusedField: Field?

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                // SECTION 1: Account Type Toggle
                Section(footer: Text(accountTypeFooter)) {
                    Toggle(isCredit ? "Debt/Credit Account" : "Cash Account", isOn: $isCredit)
                        .onChange(of: isCredit) { newValue in
                            // Don't auto-set anything - let user decide
                        }
                }
                
                // SECTION 2: Basic Info
                Section(header: Text("Account Details"), footer: Text(startingBalanceFooter)) {
                    TextField("Account Name", text: $name)
                        .focused($focusedField, equals: .name)
                        .submitLabel(.next)
                        .onSubmit {
                            focusedField = .amount
                        }
                    
                    TextField("Starting Balance", text: $initialAmountString)
                        .keyboardType(.numbersAndPunctuation) // Changed to allow math symbols
                        .focused($focusedField, equals: .amount)
                        .onSubmit {
                            focusedField = .target
                        }
                }
                
                // SECTION 3: Target Amount
                Section(header: Text(targetSectionHeader), footer: Text(targetSectionFooter)) {
                    TextField(targetPlaceholder, text: $targetAmountString)
                        .keyboardType(.numbersAndPunctuation) // Changed to allow math symbols
                        .focused($focusedField, equals: .target)
                        .onSubmit {
                            if canSave {
                                saveAccount()
                            }
                        }
                }
                
                // SECTION 4: Payment/Contribution Plan (Optional)
                if shouldShowInstallmentSection {
                    Section(header: Text(installmentSectionHeader), footer: Text(installmentSectionFooter)) {
                        Toggle("Set \(isCredit ? "Payment" : "Contribution") Plan", isOn: $hasTargetDate)
                        
                        if hasTargetDate {
                            DatePicker("Target Date",
                                     selection: $targetDate,
                                     in: Date()...,
                                     displayedComponents: .date)
                            
                            Picker("Frequency", selection: $paymentFrequency) {
                                ForEach(PaymentFrequency.allCases) { frequency in
                                    Text(frequency.rawValue).tag(frequency)
                                }
                            }
                            
                            // Preview calculation
                            if let preview = calculatePreview() {
                                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                                    Divider()
                                    
                                    HStack {
                                        Image(systemName: "calendar")
                                            .foregroundColor(.blue)
                                        Text("Payment Preview")
                                            .font(.subheadline)
                                            .fontWeight(.semibold)
                                    }
                                    
                                    HStack {
                                        Text("Payments:")
                                        Spacer()
                                        Text("\(preview.periods)")
                                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                    }
                                    .font(.caption)
                                    
                                    HStack {
                                        Text("Amount per \(paymentFrequency.rawValue.lowercased()):")
                                        Spacer()
                                        Text(preview.amountPerPeriod.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                                            .foregroundColor(isCredit ? .orange : .blue)
                                            .fontWeight(.semibold)
                                    }
                                    .font(.caption)
                                }
                                .padding(.vertical, 8)
                                .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                                .background(Color(.systemGray6))
                                .cornerRadius(8)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                // SECTION 5: Account Groups (Optional)
                if !data.accountGroups.isEmpty {
                    Section(header: Text("Add to Account Groups (Optional)"), footer: Text("This account will be included in the selected groups.")) {
                        ForEach(data.accountGroups) { group in
                            Button {
                                toggleGroupSelection(group.id)
                            } label: {
                                HStack {
                                    Image(systemName: "folder")
                                        .foregroundColor(.blue)
                                    Text(group.name)
                                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                                    Spacer()
                                    if selectedGroupIDs.contains(group.id) {
                                        Image(systemName: "checkmark")
                                            .foregroundColor(.blue)
                                            .fontWeight(.bold)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                // SECTION 6: Account Type Summary
                Section {
                    HStack {
                        Image(systemName: accountTypeIcon)
                            .foregroundColor(accountTypeColor)
                        Text("This will be a \(accountTypeName)")
                            .foregroundColor(accountTypeColor)
                    }
                }
            }
            .formToolbar(
                title: "New Account",
                canSave: canSave,
                onSave: {
                    saveAccount()
                },
                onCancel: {
                    dismiss()
                }
            )
            .decimalPadToolbar()
            .onAppear {
                focusedField = .name
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Computed Properties
    
    private var accountTypeFooter: String {
        if isCredit {
            return "Debt/Credit accounts track money you owe (credit cards, loans, installments)."
        } else {
            return "Cash accounts track money you have (bank accounts, wallets, savings)."
        }
    }
    
    private var startingBalanceFooter: String {
        if isCredit {
            return "Enter how much you currently owe (e.g., $1,250 of credit card debt)."
        } else {
            return "Enter how much money is currently in this account."
        }
    }
    
    private var targetSectionHeader: String {
        if isCredit {
            return "Spending Limit / Payoff Goal"
        } else {
            return "Savings Goal (Optional)"
        }
    }
    
    private var targetSectionFooter: String {
        let target = MathParser.calculate(targetAmountString)
        
        if isCredit {
            if target > 0 {
                return "This will be a Budget account. Track spending against this limit."
            } else {
                return "This will be a Debt account. Leave at $0 to track debt payoff, or set a monthly spending limit."
            }
        } else {
            if target > 0 {
                return "This will be a Goal account. Track your savings progress toward this target."
            } else {
                return "Leave blank for simple balance tracking."
            }
        }
    }
    
    private var targetPlaceholder: String {
        isCredit ? "0 (Debt) or Monthly Budget" : "Target Amount"
    }
    
    private var shouldShowInstallmentSection: Bool {
        let target = MathParser.calculate(targetAmountString)
        
        if isCredit {
            // Show for debt accounts (target = 0) or budgets (target > 0)
            return true
        } else {
            // Show for goals (target > 0)
            return target > 0
        }
    }
    
    private var installmentSectionHeader: String {
        isCredit ? "Payment Plan (Optional)" : "Contribution Plan (Optional)"
    }
    
    private var installmentSectionFooter: String {
        if isCredit {
            return "Set a target date to calculate required monthly payments to pay off this debt or stay within budget."
        } else {
            return "Set a target date to calculate required contributions to reach your savings goal."
        }
    }
    
    private var accountTypeIcon: String {
        let target = MathParser.calculate(targetAmountString)
        
        switch (isCredit, target > 0) {
        case (false, false): return "dollarsign.circle.fill"
        case (false, true):  return "target"
        case (true, true):   return "chart.bar.doc.horizontal.fill"
        case (true, false):  return "creditcard.and.123"
        }
    }
    
    private var accountTypeColor: Color {
        let target = MathParser.calculate(targetAmountString)
        
        switch (isCredit, target > 0) {
        case (false, false): return .primary
        case (false, true):  return .blue
        case (true, true):   return .purple
        case (true, false):  return .orange
        }
    }
    
    private var accountTypeName: String {
        let target = MathParser.calculate(targetAmountString)
        
        switch (isCredit, target > 0) {
        case (false, false): return "Debit Account"
        case (false, true):  return "Goal Account"
        case (true, true):   return "Budget Account"
        case (true, false):  return "Debt Account"
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Validation
    
    private var canSave: Bool {
        !name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty
    }
    
    // MARK: - Calculations
    
    private func calculatePreview() -> (periods: Int, amountPerPeriod: Double)? {
        guard hasTargetDate else { return nil }
        
        let initial = MathParser.calculate(initialAmountString)
        let target = MathParser.calculate(targetAmountString)
        
        let today = Date()
        guard targetDate > today else { return nil }
        
        let periods = paymentFrequency.periodsBetween(from: today, to: targetDate)
        guard periods > 0 else { return nil }
        
        let remaining: Double
        
        if isCredit {
            if target > 0 {
                // Budget account: No installment calculation needed
                return nil
            } else {
                // Debt account: Calculate payoff amount
                remaining = max(initial, 0)
            }
        } else {
            // Goal account: Calculate savings needed
            remaining = max(target - initial, 0)
        }
        
        let amountPerPeriod = remaining / Double(periods)
        return (periods, amountPerPeriod)
    }
    
    // MARK: - Actions
    
    private func toggleGroupSelection(_ groupID: UUID) {
        if selectedGroupIDs.contains(groupID) {
            selectedGroupIDs.remove(groupID)
        } else {
            selectedGroupIDs.insert(groupID)
        }
    }
    
    private func saveAccount() {
        let amount = MathParser.calculate(initialAmountString)
        let targetRaw = MathParser.calculate(targetAmountString)
        
        // Logic for Target:
        // If Debt (isCredit=true): target can be 0 (payoff goal) or >0 (budget limit).
        // If Debit (isCredit=false): target must be >0 to be a Goal. If 0, it's nil.
        
        let target: Double?
        if isCredit {
            // For credit, we store whatever the user entered (0 or higher) if the string wasn't empty
            if targetAmountString.isEmpty {
                target = nil
            } else {
                target = targetRaw
            }
        } else {
            // For debit, 0 means "Standard Debit Account", so we convert to nil
            target = targetRaw > 0 ? targetRaw : nil
        }
        
        let newAccount = Account(
            name: name,
            initialAmount: amount,
            isCredit: isCredit,
            targetAmount: target,
            targetDate: hasTargetDate ? targetDate : nil,
            paymentFrequency: hasTargetDate ? paymentFrequency : nil
        )
        
        // Add account to AppData
        data.accounts.append(newAccount)
        
        // Add to selected groups
        for groupID in selectedGroupIDs {
            if let index = data.accountGroups.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == groupID }) {
                data.accountGroups[index].memberIDs.append(newAccount.id)
            }
        }
        
        data.save()
        dismiss()
    }
}

import SwiftUI

struct TemplatesView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    
    // Controls presentation of the template usage sheet
    @State private var selectedTemplate: TransactionTemplate?
    
    // Controls presentation of the Edit sheet
    @State private var templateToEdit: TransactionTemplate?

    // MARK: - Search State
    @State private var searchText = ""
    
    // MARK: - Expansion State
    @State private var isScheduledExpanded = true
    @State private var isStandardExpanded = true

    // MARK: - Filter & Sort Logic
    var filteredTemplates: [TransactionTemplate] {
        // 1. Determine which list to start with
        let templatesToFilter: [TransactionTemplate]
        
        if searchText.isEmpty {
            templatesToFilter = data.templates
        } else {
            templatesToFilter = data.templates.filter { template in
                let nameMatch = template.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(searchText)
                let accName = accountName(for: template.accountID) ?? ""
                let accMatch = accName.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(searchText)
                let catName = categoryName(for: template.categoryID) ?? ""
                let catMatch = catName.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(searchText)
                return nameMatch || accMatch || catMatch
            }
        }
        
        // 2. Sort the resulting list Alphabetically
        return templatesToFilter.sorted {
            $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveCompare($1.name) == .orderedAscending
        }
    }
    
    // Split into groups
    var scheduledTemplates: [TransactionTemplate] {
        filteredTemplates.filter { $0.reminderSchedule != nil }
    }
    
    var standardTemplates: [TransactionTemplate] {
        filteredTemplates.filter { $0.reminderSchedule == nil }
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                // Custom Search Bar
                HStack {
                    Image(systemName: "magnifyingglass")
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    TextField("Search templates...", text: $searchText)
                }
                .padding(10)
                .background(Color(UIColor.secondarySystemBackground))
                .cornerRadius(10)
                .padding(.horizontal)
                .padding(.bottom, 10)

                List {
                    if data.templates.isEmpty {
                        Text("No templates yet. Create one from the Add Transaction form.")
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            .padding()
                    } else if filteredTemplates.isEmpty {
                        Text("No templates match your search.")
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            .padding()
                    } else {
                        // SECTION 1: STANDARD
                        if !standardTemplates.isEmpty {
                            DisclosureGroup(
                                isExpanded: $isStandardExpanded,
                                content: {
                                    ForEach(standardTemplates) { template in
                                        templateRow(template)
                                    }
                                },
                                label: { headerLabel(title: "Standard", count: standardTemplates.count, icon: "list.bullet", color: .primary) }
                            )
                        }
                        
                        // SECTION 2: WITH REMINDERS
                        if !scheduledTemplates.isEmpty {
                            DisclosureGroup(
                                isExpanded: $isScheduledExpanded,
                                content: {
                                    ForEach(scheduledTemplates) { template in
                                        templateRow(template)
                                    }
                                },
                                label: { headerLabel(title: "Scheduled", count: scheduledTemplates.count, icon: "bell.fill", color: .blue) }
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
                .listStyle(.plain) // Cleaner look with custom search bar
            }
            .navigationTitle("Templates")
            
            // Sheets
            .sheet(item: $selectedTemplate) { template in
                AddTransactionView(initialType: template.type, template: template)
                    .environmentObject(data)
            }
            .sheet(item: $templateToEdit) { template in
                EditTemplateView(template: template)
                    .environmentObject(data)
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - View Builders
    
    private func headerLabel(title: String, count: Int, icon: String, color: Color) -> some View {
        HStack {
            Label(title, systemImage: icon)
                .font(.headline)
                .foregroundColor(color)
            Spacer()
            Text("\(count)")
                .font(.caption)
                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                .background(Color(.secondarySystemBackground))
                .clipShape(Capsule())
        }
        .padding(.vertical, 4)
    }
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private func templateRow(_ template: TransactionTemplate) -> some View {
        HStack {
            // 1. The Main Template Info (Tap to use)
            Button {
                selectedTemplate = template
            } label: {
                VStack(alignment: .leading) {
                    Text(template.name)
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                    HStack {
                        Text(template.type.rawValue.capitalized)
                        if let accountName = accountName(for: template.accountID) {
                            Text("– \(accountName)")
                        }
                        if let categoryName = categoryName(for: template.categoryID) {
                            Text("– \(categoryName)")
                        }
                    }
                    .font(.subheadline)
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    
                    // Reminder Detail
                    if let schedule = template.reminderSchedule {
                        HStack(spacing: 4) {
                            Image(systemName: "bell.fill")
                                .font(.caption2)
                            Text("\(schedule.frequency.rawValue)")
                                .font(.caption2)
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(.blue)
                        .padding(.top, 2)
                    }
                }
            }
            
            Spacer()
            
            // 2. Visible Edit Button
            Button {
                templateToEdit = template
            } label: {
                Image(systemName: "slider.horizontal.3")
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    .padding(.leading, 10)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.borderless)
        }
        // Swipe Actions
        .swipeActions(edge: .leading) {
            Button {
                templateToEdit = template
            } label: {
                Label("Edit", systemImage: "pencil")
            }
            .tint(.blue)
        }
        .swipeActions(edge: .trailing, allowsFullSwipe: true) {
            Button(role: .destructive) {
                deleteSingleTemplate(template)
            } label: {
                Label("Delete", systemImage: "trash")
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Helpers
    
    private func deleteSingleTemplate(_ template: TransactionTemplate) {
        data.removeTemplate(template)
    }

    private func accountName(for id: UUID?) -> String? {
        guard let id = id else { return nil }
        return data.accounts.first(where: { $0.id == id })?.name
    }

    private func categoryName(for id: UUID?) -> String? {
        guard let id = id else { return nil }
        return data.categories.first(where: { $0.id == id })?.name
    }
}

//
//  EditTemplateView.swift
//  Pocket Ledger
//
//  Created by Zhi Zheng Yeo on 17/1/26.

import SwiftUI

struct EditTemplateView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @Environment(\.dismiss) var dismiss
    
    @State var template: TransactionTemplate
    
    // Schedule State
    @State private var enableReminder: Bool = false
    @State private var frequency: ReminderSchedule.Frequency = .daily
    @State private var reminderTime: Date = Date()
    @State private var dayOfWeek: Int = 2 // Monday
    @State private var dayOfMonth: Int = 1
    
    init(template: TransactionTemplate) {
        _template = State(initialValue: template)
        
        // Initialize state from existing schedule if present
        if let schedule = template.reminderSchedule {
            _enableReminder = State(initialValue: true)
            _frequency = State(initialValue: schedule.frequency)
            _dayOfWeek = State(initialValue: schedule.dayOfWeek ?? 2)
            _dayOfMonth = State(initialValue: schedule.dayOfMonth ?? 1)
            
            let calendar = Calendar.current
            let date = calendar.date(from: schedule.time) ?? Date()
            _reminderTime = State(initialValue: date)
        }
    }

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                Section(header: Text("Template Details")) {
                    TextField("Name", text: $template.name)
                }
                
                Section(header: Text("Smart Reminder")) {
                    Toggle("Enable Reminder", isOn: $enableReminder)
                    
                    if enableReminder {
                        Picker("Frequency", selection: $frequency) {
                            ForEach(ReminderSchedule.Frequency.allCases) { freq in
                                Text(freq.rawValue).tag(freq)
                            }
                        }
                        
                        if frequency == .weekly {
                            Picker("Day", selection: $dayOfWeek) {
                                Text("Sunday").tag(1)
                                Text("Monday").tag(2)
                                Text("Tuesday").tag(3)
                                Text("Wednesday").tag(4)
                                Text("Thursday").tag(5)
                                Text("Friday").tag(6)
                                Text("Saturday").tag(7)
                            }
                        }
                        
                        if frequency == .monthly {
                            Picker("Day of Month", selection: $dayOfMonth) {
                                ForEach(1...31, id: \.self) { day in
                                    Text("\(day)").tag(day)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        
                        DatePicker("Time", selection: $reminderTime, displayedComponents: .hourAndMinute)
                    }
                }
                
                if enableReminder {
                    Section {
                        Text("Pocket Ledger will send a notification: 'Time to record: \(template.name)'")
                            .font(.caption)
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                }
            }
            .formToolbar(title: "Edit Template") {
                save()
            } onCancel: {
                dismiss()
            }
        }
    }
    
    private func save() {
        if enableReminder {
            let components = Calendar.current.dateComponents([.hour, .minute], from: reminderTime)
            let schedule = ReminderSchedule(
                frequency: frequency,
                time: components,
                dayOfWeek: frequency == .weekly ? dayOfWeek : nil,
                dayOfMonth: frequency == .monthly ? dayOfMonth : nil
            )
            template.reminderSchedule = schedule
        } else {
            template.reminderSchedule = nil
        }
        
        data.updateTemplate(template)
        dismiss()
    }
}

import SwiftUI

struct CategoryDetailView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @AppStorage("currencySymbol") private var currencySymbol: String = "$"
    
    let category: Category
    // UPDATED: Now accepts a full range (e.g., "Last Year") instead of just one month
    let dateFilter: DateInterval
    
    var body: some View {
        List {
            // Header: Total for this period
            Section {
                VStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 8) {
                    Text(category.emoji)
                        .font(.system(size: 50))
                    
                    Text(category.name)
                        .font(.headline)
                    
                    Text(totalAmount.formattedCurrency(symbol: currencySymbol))
                        .font(.system(size: 36, weight: .bold, design: .rounded))
                        .foregroundColor(category.type == .inflow ? .green : .red)
                    
                    // Display the range cleanly
                    Text(formattedDateRange)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        .font(.subheadline)
                }
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                .padding(.vertical)
                .listRowBackground(Color.clear)
            }
            
            // Transaction List
            Section(header: Text("Transactions")) {
                if transactions.isEmpty {
                    Text("No transactions found in this period.")
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                } else {
                    ForEach(transactions) { transaction in
                        NavigationLink(destination: TransactionDetailView(transaction: transaction)) {
                            TransactionRow(transaction: transaction)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        .navigationTitle(category.name)
        .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
    }
    
    // MARK: - Logic
    
    private var transactions: [Transaction] {
        return data.transactions
            .filter { $0.categoryID == category.id }
            .filter { dateFilter.contains($0.date) } // UPDATED: Checks the full range
            .sorted { $0.date > $1.date }
    }
    
    private var totalAmount: Double {
        transactions.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.amount }
    }
    
    // Smart formatter to handle "Month" vs "Range"
    private var formattedDateRange: String {
        let calendar = Calendar.current
        let start = dateFilter.start
        let end = dateFilter.end
        
        // Check if it looks like a full month (e.g., Jan 1 to Feb 1)
        if let monthEnd = calendar.date(byAdding: .month, value: 1, to: start),
           calendar.isDate(monthEnd, equalTo: end, toGranularity: .day) &&
           calendar.component(.day, from: start) == 1 {
            return start.formatted(.dateTime.month(.wide).year())
        }
        
        // Otherwise, show range (e.g., "Jan 15 - Feb 14")
        let formatter = DateFormatter()
        formatter.dateStyle = .medium
        formatter.timeStyle = .none
        return "\(formatter.string(from: start)) - \(formatter.string(from: end.addingTimeInterval(-1)))"
    }
}

import SwiftUI

/// Form to add a new category. Users choose a name, a type (inflow or outflow)
/// and optionally provide an emoji for quick visual identification.
struct AddCategoryView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @Environment(\.dismiss) var dismiss
    @State private var name: String = ""
    @State private var type: CategoryType = .outflow
    @State private var emoji: String = ""
    
    // MARK: - Focus State
    enum Field: Hashable {
        case name
        case emoji
    }
    
    @FocusState private var focusedField: Field?

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                Section(header: Text("Name")) {
                    TextField("Category Name", text: $name)
                        .focused($focusedField, equals: .name)
                        .onSubmit {
                            // When user taps Return on Name, move to Type picker or Emoji
                            // Since Type is a Picker (not text field), skip to Emoji
                            focusedField = .emoji
                        }
                }
                Section(header: Text("Type")) {
                    Picker("Type", selection: $type) {
                        ForEach(CategoryType.allCases) { t in
                            Text(t.rawValue.capitalized).tag(t)
                        }
                    }
                    .pickerStyle(SegmentedPickerStyle())
                }
                Section(header: Text("Emoji")) {
                    TextField("Emoji (Optional)", text: $emoji)
                        .focused($focusedField, equals: .emoji)
                        .onSubmit {
                            // When user taps Return on Emoji, attempt to save
                            if canSave {
                                saveCategory()
                            }
                        }
                }
            }
            .formToolbar(
                title: "New Category",
                canSave: canSave,
                onSave: {
                    saveCategory()
                },
                onCancel: {
                    dismiss()
                }
            )
            .onAppear {
                // Auto-focus on Name field when view appears
                focusedField = .name
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Helpers
    
    private var canSave: Bool {
        !name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty
    }
    
    private func saveCategory() {
        data.addCategory(name: name, type: type, emoji: emoji)
        dismiss()
    }
}

import SwiftUI

struct ManageCategoriesView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @State private var showingAddSheet = false
    @State private var categoryToEdit: Category?
    @State private var searchText = ""
    
    // SPLIT BY TYPE + SORTED ALPHABETICALLY
    var inflowCategories: [Category] {
        let list = searchText.isEmpty
            ? data.categories.filter { $0.type == .inflow }
            : data.categories.filter { $0.type == .inflow && $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(searchText) }
        return list.sorted { $0.name.localizedStandardCompare($1.name) == .orderedAscending }
    }
    
    var outflowCategories: [Category] {
        let list = searchText.isEmpty
            ? data.categories.filter { $0.type == .outflow }
            : data.categories.filter { $0.type == .outflow && $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(searchText) }
        return list.sorted { $0.name.localizedStandardCompare($1.name) == .orderedAscending }
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        ZStack {
            List {
                // INFLOW SECTION
                if !inflowCategories.isEmpty {
                    Section(header: Text("Inflow")) {
                        ForEach(inflowCategories) { category in
                            categoryRow(category)
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                // OUTFLOW SECTION
                if !outflowCategories.isEmpty {
                    Section(header: Text("Outflow")) {
                        ForEach(outflowCategories) { category in
                            categoryRow(category)
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                // EMPTY STATE (when search returns no results)
                if inflowCategories.isEmpty && outflowCategories.isEmpty {
                    Section {
                        Text("No categories found")
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .center)
                            .padding()
                    }
                }
            }
            .searchable(text: $searchText, placement: .navigationBarDrawer(displayMode: .always))
            .navigationTitle("Categories")
            // REMOVED: Toolbar button
            .sheet(isPresented: $showingAddSheet) {
                AddCategoryView().environmentObject(data)
            }
            .sheet(item: $categoryToEdit) { category in
                EditCategoryView(category: category).environmentObject(data)
            }
            
            // ADDED: FAB for adding categories (bottom-right)
            .floatingAddButton {
                showingAddSheet = true
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Reusable Category Row
    
    @ViewBuilder
    private func categoryRow(_ category: Category) -> some View {
        HStack {
            Text(category.emoji)
            Text(category.name).foregroundColor(.primary)
            Spacer()
            // Badge removed since section headers now indicate type
        }
        .contentShape(Rectangle())
        .onTapGesture { categoryToEdit = category }
        .swipeActions(edge: .leading) {
            Button { categoryToEdit = category } label: {
                Label("Edit", systemImage: "pencil")
            }
            .tint(.blue)
        }
        .swipeActions(edge: .trailing) {
            Button(role: .destructive) { deleteCategory(category) } label: {
                Label("Delete", systemImage: "trash")
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Actions
    
    private func deleteCategory(_ category: Category) {
        if let index = data.categories.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == category.id }) {
            data.categories.remove(at: index)
            data.save()
        }
    }
}

FILE: Pocket Ledger/Views/Categories/EditCategoryView.swift

import SwiftUI

struct EditCategoryView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var data: AppData
    @Environment(\.dismiss) var dismiss
    var category: Category
    
    @State private var name: String
    @State private var emoji: String
    @State private var type: CategoryType

    init(category: Category) {
        self.category = category
        _name = State(initialValue: category.name)
        _emoji = State(initialValue: category.emoji)
        _type = State(initialValue: category.type)
    }

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                Section(header: Text("Details")) {
                    TextField("Name", text: $name)
                        .submitLabel(.next)
                    TextField("Emoji", text: $emoji)
                        .submitLabel(.done)
                }
                
                Section(header: Text("Type")) {
                    Picker("Type", selection: $type) {
                        ForEach(CategoryType.allCases) { t in
                            Text(t.rawValue.capitalized).tag(t)
                        }
                    }
                    .pickerStyle(SegmentedPickerStyle())
                }
            }
            .formToolbar(
                title: "Edit Category",
                canSave: !name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty,
                onSave: {
                    data.updateCategory(id: category.id, name: name, type: type, emoji: emoji)
                    dismiss()
                },
                onCancel: {
                    dismiss()
                }
            )
        }
    }
}
